### [1] [Derived Associative Algebras and Cyclic Cohomology of Q-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04805)
*Andrew James Bruce*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Q-流形（具有平方为零的奇向量场的超流形）框架下重新审视了Loday的导出积。通过转移分次将奇乘积解释为标准乘积，从而在每个Q-流形上构造了一个导出的（非交换）结合的\\( \mathbb{Z}_2 \\)-分次代数。作者将Connes循环上同调应用于该导出结合代数，得到了一种区别于标准上同调的新上同调理论——Q-流形的导出循环上同调，并将其导出循环上闭链解释为BV-BFV-BRST形式论中的经典BRST不变泛函，以及应用于正则叶状结构时（通过其叶状李代数胚）的广义Ruelle-Sullivan流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-流形的上同调理论主要基于奇向量场的标准微分（如BRST上同调），但缺乏一种从非交换几何和循环上同调视角构造的新工具。作者旨在通过Loday导出积和Connes循环上同调的结合，为Q-流形提供一种全新的、不同于标准上同调的理论框架，并揭示其在物理（BV-BF V-BRST形式论）和几何（叶状结构）中的解释。

Method: 首先重新解释Loday导出积：通过转移分次，将奇乘积转化为标准结合乘积，从而在每个Q-流形上构造导出的\\( \mathbb{Z}_2 \\)-分次结合代数。随后，将Connes的循环上同调理论应用于这一导出代数，定义Q-流形的导出循环上同调。所得导出循环上闭链在物理上被解释为BRST不变泛函，在几何上通过叶状李代数胚被解释为广义Ruelle-Sullivan流。

Result: 建立了Q-流形上的导出结合代数结构（源自Loday导出积），并在此代数上定义了新的导出循环上同调理论，该理论独立于标准Q-流形上同调。证明了导出循环上闭链与经典BRST不变泛函及广义Ruelle-Sullivan流的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文为Q-流形提供了基于非交换几何（循环上同调）的新上同调理论——导出循环上同调，该理论在约束系统形式论（BV-BF V-BRST）与叶状几何中均有自然应用。这一框架建立起了Q-流形、导出代数与循环上同调之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Loday's derived product is revisited in the setting of Q-manifolds, i.e., supermanifolds equipped with an odd vector field that `squares to zero'. We interpret the Grassmann odd product as a standard product upon shifting the grading, which implies the existence of a derived (noncommutative) associative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded algebra associated with any Q-manifold. We apply Connes' cyclic cohomology to the derived associative algebra, giving a new cohomology on Q-manifolds distinct from the standard cohomology: we refer to this as the derived cyclic cohomology of a Q-manifold. The derived cyclic cocycles are interpreted as classically BRST-invariant functionals within the BV--BFV--BRST formalism or generalised Ruelle--Sullivan currents when applied to regular foliations via their foliation Lie algebroids.

</details>


### [2] [Finite-Corner Reconstruction and Separation for Cones of Normal Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04889)
*Mohsen Kian,Fuad Kittaneh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为算子空间（B(H)到B(K)的规范映射）中的凸锥建立了一个"有限角框架"，其核心结构假设是有限切割-填充（cut-pad）运算下的稳定性。作者证明点-超弱闭的cut-pad稳定锥完全由其有限维角锥决定；反之，任意一致族的闭有限维角锥都有唯一的全局实现。对于非点-超弱闭的cut-pad稳定锥，可由其角锥的范数闭包精确重构其点-超弱闭包。将该重构原理与有限维分离及Choi表示结合，可证明全局锥的非成员性总能在单个有限维角上由有限维见证者检测。文中以完全正映射和可分解映射为例说明该框架，可分解情形下有限角见证者的Hermite部分为PPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子代数与量子信息中，许多关键凸锥（如完全正映射、可分解映射等）的结构本质上是无限维的，直接分析十分困难。已有研究表明这类锥在"剪切到有限维并填充回去"（cut-pad）操作下具有稳定性，但缺乏一个系统框架来揭示无限维锥与有限维角锥之间的精确对应关系，也缺乏统一的分离/见证者理论。

Method: 提出并系统研究"cut-pad稳定锥"的有限维角（finite-corner）理论。主要技术路线包括：(1) 证明点-超弱闭的cut-pad稳定锥与其有限维角锥族之间存在一一对应；(2) 对一般（非闭）cut-pad稳定锥，通过对角锥取范数闭包来重构原锥的点-超弱闭包；(3) 将有限维分离定理与Choi表示结合，构造有限维见证者来判断全局锥的非成员性；(4) 分别以完全正映射和可分解映射作为具体案例验证框架的有效性。

Result: 建立了cut-pad稳定锥的完整结构定理：点-超弱闭锥由有限维角锥一致族唯一确定；重构原理表明任意cut-pad稳定锥的闭包可由角锥的范数闭包恢复。获得了有限维见证者检测定理，即非成员性可被单个有限维角的有限维见证者捕获。在可分解映射情形下，证明了见证者可选取为Hermite部分为PPT的形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为无限维算子空间中的凸锥提供了统一的有限维归约框架，将全局性问题（如成员判定、闭包重构）化为可处理的有限维问题，为量子信息论中完全正、可分解等映射类的研究提供了新工具，并有望推广到其他具有类似阿基米德结构的锥族。

Abstract: We develop a finite-corner framework for convex cones of normal maps between \(\mathcal B(\mathcal H)\) and \(\mathcal B(\mathcal K)\). The basic structural assumption is stability under finite cut--pad operations. We prove that every point-ultraweakly closed cut--pad stable cone is completely determined by its finite-dimensional corner cones, and conversely that every coherent family of closed finite-dimensional corner cones admits a unique global realization of this type. For a cut--pad stable cone that is not necessarily point-ultraweakly closed, reconstruction from the norm closures of its corner cones yields exactly its point-ultraweak closure. Combining this reconstruction principle with finite-dimensional separation and the Choi representation, we show that non-membership in a global cone is always detected on a single finite-dimensional corner by a finite-dimensional witness. We illustrate the framework for completely positive and decomposable maps; in the decomposable case, the Hermitian parts of the finite-corner witnesses are PPT.

</details>


### [3] [Representation Theory of Canonical Commutation Relations Arising from the Quantization of the Klein-Gordon Equation with an External Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05237)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa,Itaru Sasaki,Akito Suzuki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子化大质量标量场在外势影响下的Weyl表示（正则对易关系表示），探讨质量或势函数变化时Weyl表示是否与原表示等价。问题归结为Schrödinger算子研究。结果表明：质量不同时Weyl表示不等价；质量相同时，等价与不等价的转变发生在势差衰减率-3/2处，这与散射理论中定义短程条件的-1衰减率形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在外部势场作用下量子化标量场的Weyl表示在质量或外势改变时是否保持等价性，这是量子场论中表示论的核心问题，也与散射理论中的短程条件存在有趣的对比。

Method: 将Weyl表示的等价性问题约化为Schrödinger算子的数学分析，通过研究算子谱性质来确定表示间等价与否的判别条件。

Result: （1）当质量改变时，Weyl表示必然不等价；（2）当质量相同时，等价性的转变阈值是两势函数之差的衰减率-3/2，与散射理论中短程条件的-1衰减率不同。

Conclusion: Weyl表示的等价性判据在数学上与散射理论中的短程条件不同，揭示了场论表示等价性与量子散射之间微妙的关系界限。

Abstract: We investigate the Weyl representation of the canonical commutation relations for a model describing a quantized massive scalar field under the influence of an external potential. The main problem is to determine whether the Weyl representation remains equivalent to or becomes inequivalent to the original one when the mass and/or potential are changed. This problem is reduced to the study of Schrödinger operators. It turns out that the Weyl representations are inequivalent when the masses differ. Moreover, when the masses are the same, the transition between equivalence and inequivalence occurs at the decay rate $-3/2$ of the difference between the potentials. This contrasts with the decay rate $-1$ that defines the short-range condition in scattering theory.

</details>

### [4] [Multiple zeta values in $λ$-rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04424)
*Sergey Mozgovoy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在λ-环中统一经典多重zeta值与多重q-zeta值的框架，证明了多重zeta函数定义了从拟混洗代数到λ-环的代数同态，并证明了λ-环中多重对数函数类似于经典情形的迭代积分表达式。作为应用，显式描述了正则多重q-zeta值的代数结构，计算了相应的庞加莱级数，并给出了一个小生成集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多重zeta值（MZV）和多重q-zeta值虽然有各自丰富的研究，但在统一框架下理解它们的结构仍是一个核心问题。经典MZV有著名的代数结构（拟混洗代数同态、迭代积分表示等），而多重q-zeta值是否存在类似结构以及其代数生成结构尚不完全清晰。作者希望通过λ-环这一工具建立统一视角，从而揭示两者的深层联系。

Method: 作者提出一个在λ-环中定义多重zeta值的框架，该框架将经典MZV和多重q-zeta值作为特例包含其中。核心做法是：(1) 将多重zeta函数构造为从拟混洗代数到λ-环的代数同态；(2) 借助λ-环的结构，建立多重对数函数的迭代积分型公式；(3) 利用该统一框架推导正则多重q-zeta值的代数结构，并通过庞加莱级数刻画其维数，进而构造生成元集合。

Result: 成功实现了两类多重zeta值在λ-环框架下的统一；证明了多重zeta函数的代数同态性质和多重对数函数的迭代积分公式；显式描述了正则多重q-zeta值的代数，计算了对应的庞加莱级数，并构造了一个小的生成集。

Conclusion: λ-环框架为研究经典和量子（q-）多重zeta值提供了统一的代数视角，不仅重现了经典理论中的核心结构（拟混洗代数同态、迭代积分公式），而且给出了正则多重q-zeta值的完整的代数描述和生成信息，为后续深入研究两者的关联和互推打开了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a framework for multiple zeta values in $λ$-rings that unifies both classical multiple zeta values and multiple $q$-zeta values. As in the classical case, we show that the multiple zeta function defines an algebra homomorphism from the quasi-shuffle algebra to the $λ$-ring. We also prove a formula for multiple polylogarithms in $λ$-rings, similar to the iterated integral expression in the classical case. As an application, we explicitly describe the algebra of regular multiple $q$-zeta values, compute the corresponding Poincaré series, and exhibit a small spanning set.

</details>


### [5] [SIC dimension towers via cyclotomic polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04431)
*Gary McConnell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Zauner SIC-POVM猜想在实二次域上的维度塔结构定理，将其转化为无限二维分圆阵列问题，并通过统一的复合范数关系建立了与Leopoldt猜想mod-p版本的深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIC-POVM猜想是量子信息理论中的核心开放问题，其数论表述将维度问题与实二次域的结构联系起来。作者试图建立SIC维度之间的关系，并希望将其置于更广泛的算术语境中，从而揭示Zauner猜想的中央3-对称性背后的深层数论结构。

Method: 通过引入两个参数族c_{m,k}=1-ζ_m ε^k，建立分圆域塔{K(μ_m)}上的封闭复合范数关系。将SIC维度塔{d_k=1+t_k}识别为水平m=3的二维分圆阵列{Ψ_m(t_k)}，并通过Ψ_m（ζ_m+ζ_m^{-1}的最小多项式）进行编码。利用p-adic类数公式将p进赋值与相应的p-adic L值联系起来。

Result: 证明了实二次域上的SIC维度塔可嵌入无限二维分圆阵列结构；在避开整除2mD的素数处，每一固定水平m的赋值v_p(Ψ_m(t_k))可由单一局部单位赋值精确描述，并通过p-adic类数公式与p-adic L值建立关联。

Conclusion: 该框架将Zauner的SIC-POVM猜想置于更广泛的算术语境中，为理解mod-p Leopoldt猜想提供了新视角，揭示了3-对称性与分圆多项式之间的深层结构联系。

Abstract: We prove a structure theorem for the dimension towers~$\{d_k(D)\}_{k\geq0}$ which arise in the number-theoretic formulation of Zauner's SIC-POVM conjecture over a real quadratic field~$K=\Q(\qD)$. If~$\eps$ denotes the first totally positive power of a fundamental unit of~$K$ and $t_k = \eps^k + \eps^{-k}$ the trace of its $k$-th power, then the SIC dimension tower~$\{d_k=1+t_k\}_{k\geq0}$ is the level~$m=3$ row of an infinite two-dimensional cyclotomic array~$\{Ψ_m(t_k)\}_{m\geq1,k\geq0}$ attached to~$K$, while the auxiliary factors~$(d_k+1)$ and $(d_k-3)$ are its ramified levels $m=2$ and $m=1$. Here~$Ψ_m$ denotes the minimal polynomial of~ $ζ_m+ζ_m^{-1} = 2\cos{2π/m}$. This construction arose initially from an attempt to formulate relations among SIC dimensions in $q$-algebraic terms.
  The central object is a single closed composite norm relation for the two-parameter family $c_{m,k}=1-ζ_m\eps^k$ over the cyclotomic field tower $\{K(μ_m)\}_{m\geq1}$. This framework sheds new light on the mod-$p$ analogue of Leopoldt's conjecture, by placing the central 3-symmetry of Zauner's conjecture within a broader arithmetic context. Away from the primes dividing~$2mD$, the valuations $v_p(Ψ_m(t_k))$ at every fixed level~$m$ are described exactly in terms of a single local unit valuation, which is then related, through the~$p$-adic class number formula, to the corresponding $p$-adic $L$-value.

</details>


### [6] [A counterexample to the Chung-Graham-Spiro gap-set conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04473)
*Mohsen Aliabadi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: Chung、Graham和Spiro引入了慢Fibonacci游走并用其将整数n≥2划分为两个序列：下行整数和上行整数。他们推测这两个序列的ℓ步间隙集在任意ℓ≥1时都一致，本文证明了该猜想在ℓ=4时失败，即9∈U₄但9∉D₄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Chung、Graham和Spiro关于慢Fibonacci游走划分的上下行整数序列的ℓ步间隙集一致性的猜想是否成立。

Method: 通过构造和分析这两种整数序列的局部间隙结构，寻找ℓ=4步间隙集的差异元素，并给出严格的数论证明来反驳该猜想。

Result: 证明了9属于上行整数的4步间隙集（U₄），但不属于下行整数的4步间隙集（D₄），从而推翻了原猜想在ℓ=4时的正确性。

Conclusion: Chung、Graham与Spiro提出的关于两序列ℓ步间隙集一致性的猜想并不成立，至少在ℓ=4时存在反例。

Abstract: Chung, Graham, and Spiro introduced slow Fibonacci walks and used them to partition the integers $n\ge2$ into two sequences, the down-integers and the up-integers. They studied the local spacing of these two sequences and conjectured that their $\ell$-step gap sets agree for every $\ell\ge1$. We show that the conjecture fails at $\ell=4$ by proving \[
  9\in U_4\setminus D_4 . \]

</details>


### [7] [Hilbert's Irreducibility for $\mathbb{G}_m$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04551)
*Michael Stoll,Samir Siksek*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了数域上的希尔伯特不可约性定理的细化问题。具体地，对有限个非阿基米德位组成的集合S，作者给出了S-整数单位群与可约纤维集合Red(π)的交集的显式描述。作为应用，证明了一个受Pólya-Siegel经典定理启发的结果：对于有理素数p_1,...,p_s和多项式f∈Q[x]，f(x)-p_1^{e_1}...p_s^{e_s}在无穷多个指标组下可约当且仅当f具有特定的特殊形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特不可约性定理说明可约纤维集合Red(π)包含在P^1(K)的一个薄集中，但未给出该集合的结构性刻画。本文的动机是显式地描述S-整数单位群与可约纤维交集的结构，并由此推广经典的Pólya-Siegel定理到多元指数情形。

Method: 作者通过将S-整数单位群O_S^×与Red(π)的交集进行显式刻画，建立几何与数论之间的联系。核心工具涉及覆盖空间理论、伽罗瓦作用分析以及单位群的算术性质。在应用部分，将问题归约为对f(x)减去S-单位（由给定素数生成）后多项式可约性的分类，通过分析各情形下f的因子结构给出充要刻画。

Result: 给出了O_S^× ∩ Red(π)的显式描述。作为推论，证明了：当f∈Q[x]且p_1,...,p_s为有理素数时，f(x)-p_1^{e_1}...p_s^{e_s}对无穷多个指数组(e_1,...,e_s)∈N^s可约，当且仅当f可表示为p_1^{a_1}...p_s^{a_s}g^ℓ（ℓ为素数）或f=-4p_1^{a_1}...p_s^{a_s}g^4的形式，其中g∈Q[x]，a_i为整数。

Conclusion: 论文将希尔伯特不可约性定理在S-单位集合上的行为完全分类，推广了Pólya-Siegel类型的经典结果到多素数情形的充分必要条件，深化了我们对可约纤维算术结构的理解。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a number field and $S$ a finite set of non-archimedean places. Write $\mathcal{O}_S$ for the ring of $S$-integers of $K$ and $\mathcal{O}_S^\times$ for its unit group. Let $π: X \rightarrow \mathbb{P}^1$ be a morphism of (irreducible) curves defined over $K$, and denote by $\operatorname{Red}(π)$ the set of $α\in \mathbb{P}^1(K)$ such that the fibre $π^{-1}(α)$ is reducible (i.e. the Galois action on the fibre is intransitive). Hilbert's Irreducibility Theorem asserts that $\operatorname{Red}(π)$ is contained in a thin subset of $\mathbb{P}^1(K)$. In this paper we give an explicit description of $\mathcal{O}_S^\times \cap \operatorname{Red}(π)$.
  As an application we prove the following result inspired by a classical theorem of Pólya and Siegel. Let $p_1,\dotsc,p_s$ be rational primes. Let $f \in \mathbb{Q}[x]$.Then the following are equivalent:
  - There are infinitely many tuples $(e_1,\dotsc,e_s) \in \mathbb{N}^s$ such that the polynomial $f(x)-p_1^{e_1} \cdots p_s^{e_s}$ is reducible.
  - $f=p_1^{a_1} \cdots p_s^{a_s} g^\ell$ (with $\ell$ prime) or $f=-4 p_1^{a_1} \cdots p_s^{a_s} g^4$ for some $g \in \mathbb{Q}[x]$ and some integers $a_1,\dotsc,a_s$.

</details>


### [8] [Degeneration of monodromy in the theory of Eisenstein congruences for classical groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04703)
*Sam Mundy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的Hecke理论方法来控制酉群和拟分裂经典群的p进自守伽罗瓦表示族中单值群的增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $F_1=F_2=1$, $F_{n+2}=F_{n+1}+F_n$, and define the Fibonacci zeta function by $$ Z_F(s)=\sum_{n\ge1}F_n^{-s},\qquad \operatorname{Re}s>0. $$ We determine the exact right edge of the closure of the real parts of its zeros in the half-plane of absolute convergence. If $σ_F$ is the unique solution of $$ Z_F(σ_F)=4+2\,144^{-σ_F}, $$ then $$ σ_F=0.743163398726901648\ldots, $$ $Z_F(s)\neq0$ for $\operatorname{Re}s\geσ_F$, while $$ \overline{\{\operatorname{Re}ρ:Z_F(ρ)=0,\ \operatorname{Re}ρ>0\}}=[0,σ_F]. $$ The edge is sharp in an almost-periodic sense: zeros occur with relatively dense ordinates near every admissible vertical line. We prove growing-dimensional phase locking near the edge and a Diophantine zero-free cusp, and describe the associated Jessen function and smooth mean vertical zero density. For every partial sum with $N\ge12$ we determine the corresponding exact closure edge $σ_N$, prove $σ_N\nearrowσ_F$, and obtain an exponential asymptotic for $σ_F-σ_N$. We also derive a finite-core theorem for positive integral Lucas zeta functions, with the Pell zeta function as an explicit example. Abstract: The Prouhet--Tarry--Escott (PTE) problem has many generalizations and has been studied in various algebraic domains. In this paper, we prove that finite subsets $S$ of integral domains with small additive doubling constant (but still a power of $|S|$) always contain solutions to Wright's generalization of the PTE problem: there are small subsets $A$ and $B$ of the same size such that $\sum_{a\in A} a^j=\sum_{b\in B} b^j$ for $1\le j\le k$, but not for $j=k+1$. Abstract: We introduce a new method for studying mod-$\ell$ congruences between eigenforms through the modular representation theory of $\mathrm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$. When $p\equiv \pm 1 \pmod{\ell}$, we use this theory to construct and describe extra structures on spaces of modular forms with $Γ_0(p^2)$-level at $p$ and a fixed mod-$\ell$ Galois representation. The structural results we obtain can be viewed as a refinement of classical level-raising theorems since they not only allow us to prove the existence of congruences, but also to count the number of such congruences. Our methods work equally well in the residually irreducible and residually reducible cases, allowing us to prove several new instances of congruences between Eisenstein series and cuspforms (as well as independently rederiving classical results of Mazur and more recent results of Lang--Wake). Abstract: Ferraguti and Micheli introduced the width of an $F$-set and conjectured that non-trivial $F$-sets of arbitrary width exist over every finite field. For $q\neq 2,3$, their constructions give examples of widths one and two. We prove that, for every $q\neq 2,3$ and every integer $r\geq 1$, there exists an infinite, non-trivial $F$-set in $\mathbb F_q[X]$ of width exactly $r$. Thus the finite-width part of their conjecture is settled over all such fields. The proof combines a bounded-core family of irreducible power substitutions with factorization results for $g(X^n)$, Dirichlet's theorem over $\mathbb F_q[X]$, and Kummer lifting. Core degree gives a uniform upper bound on the width, while parallel successor ladders give the required lower bound; a suitable tail of the nullity filtration then has the prescribed width.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a new framework, inspired by Schmidt's games, to study the Lagrange spectrum for simultaneous Diophantine approximation in dimension $d\geq 2$. We show the Hausdorff dimension of the set of points in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ whose best approximation constant lies in $[\varepsilon,\varepsilon(1+δ\varepsilon^{d})]$, for some constant $δ>0$, is positive, and for a slightly larger set approaches full dimension as $\varepsilon \to 0$. We also show that the box dimension of the $d$-dimensional Lagrange spectrum is bounded from below by $1-\frac{1}{d+1}$. The proof combines a novel application of the Simplex lemma near rational points with the game-theoretic framework. As an additional result we use an elementary observation to show that the naturally defined Lagrange spectrum for systems of linear forms is uncountable in the case of square matrices.

Abstract: Arithmetic terms are fixed finite compositions of additions, multiplications, subtractions, divisions with remainder and integer exponentiations. An arithmetic term in natural numbers $A$, $B$, $n$, obtained by refining the general method of the second author (arXiv:2608.22049) for elliptic curves in Weierstraß normal form, counts the solutions in $(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)^2$ for every modulus $n \geq 1$, with intermediate integers of approximately $2n^5$ binary digits instead of approximately $2n^{11}$. For a prime modulus $p \geq 17$, a second construction, based on the Hasse invariant and on the trace of Frobenius, gives a term of about thirty operations, in place of the fifty-one products of generalized geometric progressions of the general method.

*Alan Williams*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对经典多变量极值搜索（ES）架构的极小修改方案——使用有理无关的扰动频率和匹配的解调信号。对于任意局部Lipschitz静态目标函数，利用Kronecker-Weyl定理证明在固定扰动幅度下，长时间平均动力学精确等于核平滑目标的负梯度，并通过一般平均理论建立了实用全局一致渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极值搜索通常被解释为梯度下降的近似，但这种解释对于非光滑目标函数在连续时间多变量情形下并不清晰。作者希望提供一种对非光滑目标也适用的极值搜索方法，并给出严格的数学解释和稳定性保证。

Method: 对经典多变量扰动-解调架构提出最小修改：采用有理无关的扰动频率与匹配的解调信号。由于有理无关频率使扰动和解调信号非周期化，采用一般平均理论而非周期平均理论。利用Kronecker-Weyl定理证明长时间平均动力学恰为核平滑目标的负梯度，并推导出联系扰动占据密度、解调信号和平滑核的一般匹配条件。

Result: 证明了若平滑目标的梯度流全局一致渐近稳定，则极值搜索动力学具有实用全局一致渐近稳定性；推导出了一般匹配条件并得到一系列替代设计方案。数值示例包括非光滑目标函数（可解释为非线性规划的罚函数）以及Rastrigin函数（平滑消除了所有不期望的局部极小值）。

Conclusion: 该方案为多变量非光滑目标的极值搜索提供了严格的理论基础，将经典ES解释推广到非光滑情形，同时提供了稳定的保证和灵活的设计空间。

Abstract: Classical extremum seeking (ES) is commonly interpreted as approximating gradient descent, but this interpretation is less clear for nonsmooth objectives in the continuous-time multivariable setting. We propose a minimal modification of the classical multivariable perturbation--demodulation architecture: rationally independent perturbation frequencies and matched demodulation signals. For any locally Lipschitz static objective, the Kronecker--Weyl theorem shows that, at every fixed perturbation amplitude, the long-time averaged dynamics are exactly the negative gradient of a kernel-smoothed objective. Because rationally independent frequencies render the perturbation and demodulation signals nonperiodic, we employ general averaging theory rather than periodic averaging theory. If the gradient flow of the smoothed objective is globally uniformly asymptotically stable, then the ES dynamics are practically globally uniformly asymptotically stable. We also derive a general matching condition relating the perturbation occupation density, demodulation signal, and smoothing kernel, yielding a family of alternative designs. Numerical examples include a nonsmooth objective function, which may be interpreted as the penalty function of a nonlinear program, and the Rastrigin function, for which smoothing eliminates all undesired local minima.

</details>

<details>

Method: （1）建立两位风险中性委托人竞争雇佣单一代理人的博弈框架；（2）将博弈的纳什均衡与个体委托-代理签约问题进行关联分析；（3）引入基于二分法的数值算法，通过定位委托人价值函数的符号变化来近似纳什均衡；（4）在Renner和Schmedders（2015）的多项式框架下，用指数、对数和CRRA效用函数进行数值示例验证。

Result: 成功刻画了竞争性委托-代理博弈的纳什均衡，建立了均衡与个体签约问题的联系，并通过二分法数值算法在多种效用函数（指数、对数、CRRA）下完成了均衡的近似计算与示例展示。

Conclusion: 该工作为多委托人竞争环境下委托-代理问题提供了理论刻画和高效数值求解方法，表明竞争结构下的均衡可以通过联动个体问题与选择偏差结构来分析，二分法算法在多项式框架下具有良好的适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces and studies a Principal-Agent competition model where two risk-neutral Principals compete to hire a single Agent. In this framework, the Agent chooses to work exclusively for one Principal, making the Agent's reservation utility endogenous as it is directly tied to the competing offers. We characterize the Nash equilibria of the resulting game by linking them to the individual Principal-Agent contracting problems and analyzing the Agent's selection bias. Furthermore, we introduce a numerical algorithm based on the bisection method to approximate the Nash equilibria by locating the sign changes of the Principals' value functions. The procedure is illustrated through several examples involving exponential, logarithmic, and constant relative risk aversion (CRRA) utility functions within the polynomial framework of Renner and Schmedders (2015).

</details>

*Siyi Wang,Kun Huang,Lei Xu,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究时变网络上的分布式风险规避凸优化问题，目标为合作最小化各智能体局部CVaR目标的平均值。智能体仅能获得局部损失函数的噪声评估，因此提出一个零阶算法，利用采样损失构造经验CVaR估计及梯度估计。在凸性和Lipschitz连续假设下证明智能体达到渐近一致，并建立有限时间期望次优界；在递减步长和固定样本量下，局部最后一次迭代几乎必然收敛到公共最优点。该分布式界与集中式基准的参数依赖相匹配，仿真验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统常在不确定性下运行，仅最小化期望损失可能忽视罕见但严重的事件（尾部风险）。为此需要考虑风险规避的优化，但各智能体无法直接获取CVaR目标或其梯度，只能获得噪声损失评估，这促使开发不依赖梯度的零阶分布式算法。

Method: 提出一种零阶分布式算法：智能体通过采样损失构造经验CVaR估计及对应的梯度估计（利用平滑技术），在每次迭代中结合邻居决策进行局部更新。采用递减步长和固定样本量，在时间变化网络上进行信息交换与协同优化。

Result: 证明智能体达到精确渐近一致性；建立加权遍历迭代的有限时间期望次优界；在递减步长下局部最后迭代几乎必然收敛至公共最优点，且极限期望CVaR差距由平滑误差和有限样本误差界定。分布式界的参数依赖与集中式基准匹配。传感器网络估计仿真验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该零阶分布式算法能有效解决只有噪声损失评估下的分布式CVaR优化问题，其收敛性能与集中式基准相当，为分布式系统中风险规避决策提供了理论保障和实用方法。

Abstract: Distributed systems often operate under uncertainty, where minimizing expected loss may overlook rare but severe events. This paper studies a distributed risk-averse convex optimization problem in which agents cooperatively minimize the average of local conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) objectives over a time-varying network. Each agent has access only to noisy evaluations of its local loss function, rather than to its CVaR objective or gradient. We therefore develop a zeroth-order algorithm that uses sampled losses to construct empirical CVaR estimates and their gradient estimates. At each iteration, agents combine neighboring decisions and perform a local update. Under convexity and Lipschitz continuity assumptions, we prove that the agents reach exact asymptotic consensus. We also establish a finite-time expected suboptimality bound for the weighted ergodic iterate. With diminishing step sizes and fixed sample sizes, the local last iterates converge almost surely to a common optimum, and their limiting expected CVaR gap is bounded in terms of the smoothing and finite-sample errors. This distributed bound matches the parameter dependence of the centralized benchmark provided in this paper. Finally, simulations on a distributed sensor network estimation problem illustrate the efficacy of the method.

</details>

*Amin Ghodousian,Mohammad Sedigh Chopannavaz*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持常涉及异构测量、专家评估和基于指南的知识，这些信息与临床决策之间的关系往往是渐进的而非纯二元的。现有方法难以在统一优化模型中同时表示有利和不利的临床关系，且缺乏严格的数学机制来处理模糊临床关系到可行且优化的决策推荐的转化。

Method: 该框架基于双极模糊关系优化：1) 用正、负模糊关系矩阵及模糊需求向量联合表示有利与不利的临床关系；2) 通过决策变量及其补集在关系约束中的同时参与，在统一优化模型中融入双极临床信息；3) 采用最小t-范数提供非补偿的瓶颈型交互解释；4) 推导临床容许区间与有效证据激活集进行可行性分析与系统约简；5) 将完整可行集表示为有限个临床推荐区域的并集，针对每个容许的临床证据分配函数构建区域最优候选，通过有限候选比较获得全局最优推荐。

Result: 该方法通过数值CDSS示例验证，结果表明双极模糊关系优化为将模糊临床关系信息转化为可行且优化的决策支持推荐提供了结构化且数学严谨的机制。

Conclusion: 双极模糊关系优化提供了一种系统的数学框架，能够有效地将模糊临床关系信息转化为可行且优化的临床决策支持推荐，兼具可行性与最优性保证。

Abstract: Clinical decision support frequently involves heterogeneous measurements, expert assessments, and guideline-based knowledge whose relationships to clinical decisions are gradual rather than purely binary. This paper develops a clinical decision-support framework based on bipolar fuzzy relational optimization, in which favorable and unfavorable clinical relationships are represented jointly through positive and negative fuzzy relational matrices together with a fuzzy requirement vector. The components may be nonconvex, and no componentwise Hessian continuity or separate bounded-iterate assumption is required. More generally, a $ν$-Hölder-continuous average Hessian adds only $\widetilde O(n^{1+ν}T^{-2-2ν})$, so every $ν\ge 1/2$ preserves the quadratic rate. Abstract: We study a class of McKean-Vlasov control problems with discrete path dependence. The coefficients and cost functional may depend on finitely many past values of the controlled state, observed at fixed deterministic times, and on their joint law. We establish well-posedness of the controlled state equation and derive necessary and sufficient optimality conditions through a stochastic Pontryagin maximum principle. The adjoint process is characterized by a backward stochastic differential equation with jumps at fixed observation times. Each jump represents the conditional sensitivity of future costs with respect to the corresponding observed state and its distribution. In the linear-quadratic case, we prove global solvability of the resulting forward-backward system by combining a continuation argument with a mean-field Riccati reduction. We finally discuss an application to time-series generation, where the terminal cost is given by a kernel-based discrepancy between the law of the sampled path and a target path distribution.

Abstract: Blind random search repeatedly draws a candidate point and replaces the current estimate whenever the candidate has a lower loss. In the absence of noise, the true loss is observed directly. It decreases strictly at every accepted update and is monotone nonincreasing over all iterations. Measurement noise can make a worse candidate appear better and thereby break this monotonicity. To recover almost sure convergence under noise, we incorporate averaging and thresholding into the original decision criterion. These two classical tools are coupled. As the sample sizes grow, the positive threshold shrinks at a matched rate. These modifications allow blind random search to recover eventual monotonicity of the true loss under noisy measurements and to converge almost surely.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本文采用凸二次成本函数来同时建模自我影响与交叉影响。在每一期内，由一个基于波动率、成交量和相关性预测构造的半正定矩阵将不同合约间的交易耦合起来（横向耦合）；随后通过一个幂律衰减核将不同时期的交易联系起来（纵向耦合）。模型在规划期内流动性变化时，仍能证明其成本函数不产生价格操纵（即不存在无成本的反复买卖获利空间）。

Abstract: Transaction costs can make or break a trading strategy, particularly in relative-value trading of commodity and macro markets, where edges are a few basis points. Price impact is a central component of transaction cost. Price impact models usually include self-impact (a trade in a contract moves that contract's price) but omit two well-documented effects: cross-impact (a trade in one contract also moves the prices of related contracts) and transient impact (price impact decays over time, so an unwind recovers part of the entry cost). A model without these effects overprices the impact of relative-value trades, whose correlated legs are built and unwound over days, and so forgoes potentially profitable trades. This paper models both effects with a convex quadratic cost. In each period, a positive semidefinite matrix built from volatility, volume, and correlation forecasts couples trades across contracts. A power-law decay kernel then couples trades across periods. The resulting cost admits no price manipulation even when liquidity varies over the planning horizon. The model is demonstrated empirically on calendar spread trading of crude oil futures around the commodity index roll.

Abstract: In this paper, we study games involving a continuum of heterogeneous players in the discrete-time setting with finite state spaces and continuous action spaces. Classical minimum-weight CNF satisfiability is recovered as a crisp special case, independently of the selected t-norm, and an optimal Boolean solution is shown to exist in that specialization even though the proposed formulation uses continuous variables. For the graded problem, clause satisfaction is characterized through admissible variable domains and effective clause-witness sets. This structure leads to an exact clause-witness branch-and-bound method with preprocessing, propagation, lower-point closure, and a disjoint-witness packing bound. Computational experiments verify the solver against explicit witness enumeration, mixed-integer baselines, and external SATLIB formulas; on a focused graded ablation set, the packing bound reduces the median number of explored nodes by about 45 percent relative to the single-row witness bound.

Abstract: We introduce a nonlocal regularization of optimal transport that bridges Kantorovich and Monge formulations. The regularization penalizes oscillations through an interaction kernel singular near the diagonal. We show that, for sufficiently strong singularities, every finite-energy transport plan is induced by a map, yielding equivalence between the regularized Kantorovich and Monge problems. For fractional kernels, the regularizer reduces to a fractional Sobolev seminorm that can allow jump discontinuities, leading to existence results for broad classes of source and target measures. We derive first-order optimality conditions and analyze the vanishing-regularization limit. The regularized functionals $Γ$-converge to the classical Kantorovich problem, while the first-order $Γ$-limit selects, whenever they exist, finite-energy Monge minimizers with minimal nonlocal energy. When no such minimizer exists, we identify a different asymptotic regime and establish a sharp scaling law in a prototypical mass-splitting example. Finally, after lifting the problem to the product of transport plans, we derive a dual formulation using the cone of copositive functions and propose a fixed-point numerical scheme illustrating the regularization.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场博弈（MFG）的均衡通常不唯一，需要对均衡概念进行精炼以筛选更具经济合理性的解。受经典博弈论中颤抖手完美精炼的启发，作者希望为随机平均场博弈引入一种在无紧致控制集和无界系数假设下仍然成立的精炼概念，且该精炼应能实际区分不同的均衡。

Method: 通过受控鞅问题（controlled martingale problems）的松弛化框架定义松弛MFG均衡。完美性定义为两类联合控制-状态律的公共Wasserstein极限：第一类为针对其生成的状态律具有全支撑且可容许的种群扰动；第二类为对这些被扰动状态律的精确最优响应。可容许条件约束扰动的控制与状态分量不能独立变化。证明策略包括：在紧凑有界情形下使用扰动不动点论证；在一般情形下通过截断、一致矩估计、紧致性以及受控鞅问题的稳定性定理来延拓结果。 

Result: 在无需紧致控制集或有界系数的宽松假设下，证明了颤抖手完美均衡的存在性。并给出一个具有恰好两个均衡的一维模型，其中仅有一个均衡满足颤抖手完美条件，证明该精炼具有真正的选择力而并非空洞条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机平均场博弈提供了一种可计算的、经济上可辩护的均衡精炼工具，扩展了现有颤抖手精炼的适用范围（无需紧致控制或有界系数），并通过具体反例证明了其选择力。后续可探索该精炼在数值均衡选择或动态多均衡筛选中的实际应用。

Abstract: We introduce an admissible trembling-hand refinement for stochastic mean field games formulated through relaxed controlled martingale problems. A relaxed MFG equilibrium is perfect if it is the common Wasserstein limit of two sequences of joint control-state laws. The first consists of full-support population perturbations admissible for the state laws they generate; the second consists of exact optimal responses to those perturbed state laws. This admissibility condition prevents the control and state coordinates of a perturbation from being varied independently. Under the standing continuity, growth, and coercivity assumptions, we prove existence without requiring compact controls or bounded coefficients. The proof combines a perturbed fixed-point argument in the compact-bounded case with truncation, uniform moment estimates, compactness, and stability of controlled martingale problems. A one-dimensional model with exactly two relaxed mean field game equilibria shows that the refinement is genuinely selective: only one equilibrium is trembling-hand perfect.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者提出新的对偶方法：将原随机最优控制问题转化为一个以某一布朗运动路径实现为参数的路径依赖最优控制问题，从而恢复马尔可夫性；随后利用粗糙路径理论求解该路径依赖问题，得到粗糙二阶HJB方程，并在适当的粘性意义下给出解。

Abstract: We develop novel duality methods for stochastic optimal control problems under two sources of randomness and non-Markovian random coefficients adapted to just one of them. The Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation is a second-order backward stochastic partial differential equation. Our duality theory provides an alternative description of the random value function in terms of a suitable pathwise optimal control problem parameterized by the realizations of one of the Brownian motions. This allows us to regain Markovianity using the theory of rough optimal control problems, leading to rough second order HJB equations that we solve in a suitable viscosity sense.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出OR-Clarify基准测试和InterOPT框架，用于解决大语言模型在从自然语言描述构建优化模型时，面对不完整问题规格的澄清决策问题。OR-Clarify通过模拟用户交互评估代理的澄清能力，InterOPT采用两阶段框架识别公式化关键缺口并指导提问或停止，实验证明其在选择题场景下显著优于基线。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出OR-Clarify基准测试，每个任务包含部分公开问题描述和隐藏的结构化槽位，通过有界交互模拟用户进行评测；支持开放式和选择题式澄清，评估槽位恢复、停止行为、隐性假设和交互成本。同时提出InterOPT两阶段框架：第一阶段识别未解决的公式化关键缺口，第二阶段基于缺口决定继续提问或停止。

Result: 选择题式实验中，InterOPT在精确槽位恢复上大幅优于所有基线；开放式实验中，其表现与强先验方法相当。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to formulate optimization models from natural-language problem descriptions, yet realistic operations research (OR) requests are often incomplete: missing objectives, constraints, or business rules can change the resulting mathematical program. Existing evaluations largely assume a complete specification and therefore overlook whether an agent knows when clarification is needed before modeling. We introduce OR-Clarify, a benchmark for pre-formulation clarification. Each task presents a partial public problem description, withholds structured hidden slots, and evaluates agents through bounded interaction with a simulated user. The benchmark supports both openended and choice-based clarification, and measures slot recovery, stopping behavior, silent assumptions, and interaction cost. We further propose Interactive Optimization (InterOPT), a two-stage framework that identifies unresolved formulation-critical gaps and uses them to guide whether to ask the next question or to stop. In our choice-based experiments, InterOPT substantially outperforms all baselines in exact slot recovery; in the open-ended setting, it remains competitive with strong prior methods. Together, OR-Clarify and InterOPT reframe OR assistance as a selective completeness decision: clarify when needed, stop when ready, and quantify what remains missing.

</details>

### [39] [FailureSpot: Label-Efficient Timestamp-Level Failure Detection for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04277)
*Jie Ma,Zongxi Liu,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的VLA失败检测框架，利用未标记的动作块构造弱监督信号，并结合主动学习选择最有信息量的轨迹进行时间戳级标注，从而在减少标注成本的同时提升时间戳级和轨迹级的失败检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在长时程任务执行中可能不可预测地失败，需要可靠的失败检测机制。现有方法要么依赖视觉模型（只能在错误动作发生后检测），要么使用在VLA内部表示上训练的轻量级主动检测器。然而这些主动方法通常使用轨迹级标签进行监督，导致不成功轨迹中正常的失败前行为被错误标记为失败，造成标签噪声，限制了轨迹级检测精度和时间戳级失败定位能力。

Method: 提出一种数据高效的框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用未标记的VLA动作块构建动作派生的弱监督信号，捕捉异常模式（如不一致的连续动作块、冻结/空闲动作、激进的随机运动）；(2) 通过主动学习仅选择最不确定的轨迹进行时间戳级标注，并用这些信息量丰富的标签微调检测器。

Result: 在多个VLA策略上的实验表明，该方法同时提升了时间戳级和轨迹级的失败检测性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but they can still fail unpredictably during long-horizon execution, making reliable failure detection essential for safe deployment. Existing methods either rely on visual models that typically detect failures only after erroneous actions have occurred, or use lightweight proactive detectors trained on VLA internal representations. However, these proactive methods are often supervised with trajectory-level labels, causing normal pre-failure behavior in unsuccessful trajectories to be incorrectly labeled as failure. This supervision mismatch introduces label noise and limits both trajectory-level detection accuracy and precise timestamp-level failure localization. In this work, we study fine-grained timestamp-level VLA failure detection while addressing the cost of dense annotation. We propose a data-efficient framework that first leverages unlabeled VLA action chunks to construct action-derived weak supervision signals, capturing abnormal patterns such as inconsistent consecutive chunks, frozen or idle actions, and aggressive random motions. We then use active learning to select only the most uncertain trajectories for timestamp-level annotation and fine-tune the detector with these informative labels. Experiments across multiple VLA policies show that our method improves both timestamp-level and trajectory-level failure detection performance.

</details>

*Chenyu Su,Zhaolong Shen,Yuan Qian,Chen Qian,Rui Zhang,Feng Yan,Weixing Chen,Fei Zhang,Jiamin Wang,Shuang Cong,Weiwei Shang*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在四类别的九个高精度化学任务和四种机器人形态上进行广泛评估，VLA-Precision达到98.3%的平均成功率，每任务仅需45.8分钟，27.6秒的回合以VLA和RL基线速度的1.2倍和1.8倍运行。吞吐量和计算效率提升最高达10.9倍。

Conclusion: VLA-Precision通过ACoB算法解决价值信号不可靠导致的策略漂移问题，通过ACoB-Stream架构缓解大型VLA的开销瓶颈，在真实世界精密操纵任务中实现了高成功率与高训练效率，验证了在线RL对VLA后训练的有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models enable broad manipulation but remain unreliable in tasks demanding precision and repeatability. Applying real-world online reinforcement learning (RL) to VLA post-training enables autonomous trial-and-error improvement beyond demonstrations alone, but exposes two bottlenecks: 1) unreliable value signals can induce policy drift; 2) large-VLA overhead constrains throughput and sample efficiency. To address these challenges, we present VLA-Precision, an efficient real-world online RL framework featuring the Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping (ACoB) algorithm and the ACoB-Stream architecture. Specifically, ACoB establishes asymmetric co-bootstrapping across timescales: early intervention-guided behavioral learning rapidly improves policy performance while enhancing online experience quality. As autonomous experience accumulates, global return propagation and local preference ranking progressively calibrate value estimates, yielding relative action advantages for reference-regularized policy improvement while suppressing drift. To enable ACoB on large VLAs, we develop ACoB-Stream, a closed-loop experience--policy architecture that establishes invariant-state decoupling and on-demand streaming as design principles, delivering up to 10.9$\times$ improvements in throughput and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations on nine high-precision chemistry tasks across four categories and four robot embodiments show that VLA-Precision achieves 98.3\% mean success rate in 45.8 min/task, with 27.6 s episodes running at 1.2$\times$ and 1.8$\times$ the speeds of VLA and RL baselines. Resources are available at https://vla-precision.github.io.
