# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [One-Shot Screening of Simulated Systems for Acceptability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04400)
*Jinbo Zhao,David J. Eckman*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于设计面向有限模拟系统集合的筛选程序（ranking-and-selection问题）。该框架基于对未知问题实例的置信区域（而非性能比较）来决定保留或淘汰系统，能保证以高概率返回全部可接受系统或每个可接受系统，并支持多种可接受性定义（如随机约束可行性和多目标最优性）。所研究的框架子类可产生简单高效的筛选程序，通常比现有方法具有更低的时间复杂度，且支持并行化而不损失筛选能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的筛选程序大多基于系统性能的估计比较来设计，缺乏一个统一的、通用的方法来处理各种ranking-and-selection问题（包括可行性约束、单目标或多目标最优性等不同定义的可接受性），且现有方法的时间复杂度较高。

Method: 提出一个通用筛选框架，核心思想是用置信区域刻画未知问题实例，若一个系统在某些位于置信区域内的响应向量配置下具有可接受性能，则保留该系统。该框架通过选择不同的保留规则来保证以高概率返回全部可接受系统或每个可接受系统。进一步研究该框架的一个子类，该子类能生成简单、计算高效且支持并行化的筛选程序。

Result: 该框架所导出的筛选程序通常具有比现有方法更低阶的时间复杂度，且自然支持并行化且不损失筛选能力。数值实验验证了程序的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为ranking-and-selection问题中的筛选程序设计提供了一种新颖的、基于置信区域的统一视角，能够涵盖多种可接受性定义，并兼顾了理论保证、计算效率和并行化的可实现性。

Abstract: We introduce a general-purpose framework for designing screening procedures for problems featuring a finite set of simulated systems, a.k.a. ranking-and-selection problems. The framework offers a novel perspective on screening in which decisions to retain or eliminate systems are based on confidence regions for the unknown problem instance rather than comparisons of estimated performances. Specifically, a system is retained if it has acceptable performance under some plausible configuration of response vectors contained in the confidence region. This perspective facilitates the design of procedures that guarantee to return either all acceptable systems, or each acceptable system, with high probability and accommodates many well-studied definitions of acceptability, including feasibility with respect to stochastic constraints and optimality with respect to one or more objectives. We further study a subclass of the framework that yields simple and computationally efficient screening procedures that often have lower-order time complexity than existing methods and naturally supports parallelization without loss of screening power. We demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the procedures through numerical experiments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化和日益严重的野火威胁着人类和生态系统，但针对野火对农作物影响的研究甚少。除直接效应（农业工人健康风险和作物损伤）外，烟雾还可能影响作物产量和品质，因此有必要量化野火烟雾对作物生产的实际影响。

Method: 以加州北部为研究区域，使用统计回归模型量化颗粒物（particulate matter）与酿酒葡萄产量的关系，模型纳入了多种气候和空气质量预测变量。

Result: 在三个研究地点中的两个（Sonoma和Napa），颗粒物与温度共同成为显著的预测变量。最终模型的R²值分别为Sonoma 0.79和Napa 0.66，表明野火烟雾导致的空气质量恶化与酿酒葡萄产量降低存在关联，产量控制因素从纯气候主导转向野火与气候共同主导。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了野火烟雾对葡萄产量的负面影响，为下一代纳入野火威胁的农业产量模型铺平了道路，有助于农业系统应对日益增长的野火风险。

Abstract: Climate change and increasingly severe wildfires pose significant threats to both people and ecosystems, yet little research has focused on the impacts of wildfires on crops. In addition to direct effects such as health risks to agricultural workers and crop damage, there is evidence that smoke may affect crop productivity and quality. This study aims to analyze the effects of wildfire smoke on crop production in Northern California. We quantified the relationship of particulate matter on grape yields using statistical regression models with a wide range of different climate and air quality predictor variables. Our analysis identifies an association between air quality deterioration due to wildfire smoke emissions and reduction in wine grape yields. For two of the three study sites, particulate matter emerged as a significant predictor variable in combination with temperature. Final models for Sonoma and Napa had R2 values of 0.79 and 0.66 respectively, suggesting a shift from climate only-controlled yields to wildfire and climate-controlled yields. This work sets the stage for the next generation of agronomic models that incorporate the growing threat of wildfire on agriculture production systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麻疹是一种高传染性、高致死率的病毒，且在美国面临失去消除状态的风险。理解其传播模式（包括季节性）对预测、预防和公共卫生准备至关重要，因此亟需更有效的周期检测方法来分析历史疫情数据。

Method: 提出一种新颖的"变带宽周期块自助法"（VBPBB），该方法通过抑制噪声和干扰信号来增强周期成分的检测能力，与现有方法相比在统计功效和效率上更为优越。将该方法应用于历史纽约市麻疹病例数据以识别显著周期成分。

Result: VBPBB方法在历史纽约市麻疹病例数据中发现了多个显著的周期成分，而这些周期成分是其他现有方法无法识别的。这些新发现为理解麻疹传播规律提供了更深层的见解。

Conclusion: 该方法具有可重用性，不仅适用于麻疹，也可用于其他疾病的分析；同时研究结果为疫苗接种率持续下降可能导致的麻疹疫情后果提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Measles, a highly contagious, deadly virus, is at risk of losing its eradication status in the Unites States. Understanding the pattern, including seasonality, of measles could provide great benefit for forecasting, prevention, and public health preparedness as the virus re-emerges. The novel Variable Band-pass Periodic Block Bootstrap (VBPBB), which suppresses noise and interfering signals, is more efficient and statistically powerful to find periodic components compared to other existing methods. Using this method, we have found several significant periodic components in historical New York City measles cases which other methods were incapable of identifying. These findings give us a reusable method for understanding measles and other diseases and greater insight into what may occur should measles vaccine rates continue to fall.

</details>


### [77] [Pricing rides as option contracts: guarantees and memberships under travel-time uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04618)
*Mohamad Elmasri,Mingze Li,Yunran Wei*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究网约车平台里程/票价保障（upfront price guarantee）产品的公平保费定价问题。通过将网约车预定价分解为预期票价与保险索赔溢价之和，利用旅程时间分布的最新建模（度量图上的周期平稳布朗运动），推导出基于总体和路线条件旅程时间律的闭式保费公式，并提出稀疏数据下的采样器。基于2014年魁北克市GPS数据验证显示，保证保费通常低于票价的5%，路线条件化可减半保费并降低尾部风险，同时提出对滥用（adverse selection）免疫的加性补贴设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网约车平台在出行发生前（甚至提前数天）就必须承诺票价，但实际费用取决于当时仍具不确定性的出行时间。这种"提前锁定价格"本质上是一种保险产品，需要合理计算保障溢价。现有研究缺乏将金融或有权益定价理论与现代旅行时间分布建模结合起来的框架，也无法覆盖从即时叫车到预约拼车、会员及通勤产品等不同信息粒度场景的统一定价方法。

Method: 作者将提前报价分解为期望票价与保险索赔（即实际票价与承诺票价的差额）两者的和，采用或有权益定价（contingent-claim pricing）方法计算公平保费。在旅行时间分布方面，采用将渐近旅行时间分布建模为度量图（metric graph）上携带周期平稳（cyclostationary）旅行时间过程的布朗运动的最新方法，从而推导出总体律（population law）和路线条件律（route-conditional law）下的闭式保费公式。针对稀疏数据场景，设计了路线采样器和旅行时间采样器，并在2014年魁北克市GPS出行数据上进行样本外验证。

Result: 在2014年魁北克市GPS数据上，采样器能够复现实证出行时间分布。研究发现：保证保费通常很便宜，一般低于票价的5%；基于已实现路线进行条件化可使保费大约减半，同时显著降低尾部风险；通过分析会员滥用行为，发现逆向选择恰好沿着价格未条件化的维度发生，由此提出设计原则——若补贴是票价的可加部分（如每单固定折扣），则边际激励不变，此类补贴天然免疫滥用，且其成本存在确定性的闭式解。

Conclusion: 本文为网约车票价保障产品提供了从随机金融定价视角出发的统一理论框架，证明此类保险保费在实践中很低（通常<5%），并提出了兼具理论严谨性和实际可操作性的路线条件定价方法及抗滥用补贴设计原则，为平台的价格保障与补贴产品设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Modern ride-share platforms must commit to a price before a trip is taken, yet the realized fare depends on travel time that is uncertain at the moment of sale, which can occur days in advance. The upfront price thus decomposes into the expected fare and the premium on an insurance claim whose payoff is the shortfall between the realized and promised prices. This work computes the fair premium for such a claim, following from contingent-claim pricing. Recent advances in travel-time distribution modeling cast the asymptotic distribution of travel time as a Brownian motion on a metric graph carrying a cyclostationary travel-time process. We thus derive closed-form premiums under both a population and a route-conditional travel-time law. A single pricing equation then covers products of increasing informational difficulty, such as on-demand and scheduled rides and capped-membership and commuter products. To price on sparse data, we develop route and travel-time samplers and validate them out of sample on 2014 Quebec City GPS trips, where they reproduce the empirical trip-time distribution. We show that guarantee premiums are inexpensive, typically under 5% of the fare. Conditioning on the realized route roughly halves the premium while reducing tail risk. Analyzing membership abuse, we show that adverse selection acts precisely along the dimension the price fails to condition on, yielding a design principle: subsidies additive in the fare, such as a fixed per-ride discount, leave marginal incentives unchanged and are abuse-proof by construction, with deterministic closed-form cost.

</details>


### [78] [Multi-Fidelity Gaussian Processes for Translational Modelling of Clinical Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05007)
*Isaac S. Hayden,Alicia D'Souza,Steven Niederer,Sarah Filippi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多保真度高斯过程的统计机器学习框架，将动物实验视为低保真度但信息量丰富的近似，用于桥接动物与人类实验之间的差距，实现跨物种外推和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转化医学中动物实验与人体实验之间存在巨大鸿沟，受限于成本高昂、异质数据整合困难，以及减少动物实验与降低人类受试者风险之间的平衡需求。

Method: 使用多保真度高斯过程，将动物研究建模为低保真度的信息源，人类实验为高保真度目标，同时学习跨物种相似性和非线性暴露-反应关系，支持跨物种外推与不确定性量化。

Result: 在模拟的药物诱导QT间期延长场景和真实的自律神经阻断下心率变化临床数据上均进行了验证，表明该框架能以零额外实验成本整合体外药理学、动物实验和人类数据，并支持跨化合物迁移学习。

Conclusion: 该框架可在无需额外实验的情况下，仅凭体外研究发现即可为新药物生成预测和不确定性量化，支持动物实验的替代、减少和优化（3R原则），加速药物研发决策。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between animal and human experiments remains a major challenge in translational medicine, particularly in early drug development. Progress is constrained by financial cost, the difficulty of integrating heterogeneous in vitro and in vivo data, and the desire to reduce the use of animal testing balanced against minimising the risk to human participants.
  We present a statistical machine learning framework using multi-fidelity Gaussian processes, in which animal studies are considered as lower fidelity but informative approximations to human experiments. This allows cross-species similarities and nonlinear exposure-response relationships to be learned simultaneously, enabling principled extrapolation between species while quantifying uncertainty. By leveraging information from multiple experimental fidelities, our method improves estimation of clinically relevant quantities of interest and supports the replacement, reduction, and refinement of in vivo testing.
  We first illustrate this approach in a simulated scenario, before validating it on real clinical data. We simulate data for drug-induced QT-interval prolongation, a key cardiac safety assessment required for regulatory approval. This framework provides a probabilistic surrogate capable of integrating in vitro pharmacology, animal experiments and human data within a unified statistical model. Crucially, it achieves this at no additional experimental cost while also enabling transfer learning across compounds. As a result, predictions and uncertainty quantification for new drugs can be generated from in vitro findings alone, providing additional efficiency gains and accelerating decision making. For validation, we use a clinical dataset measuring change in heart rate under autonomic blockade, which represents some of the challenges commonly found in multi-species datasets.

</details>


### [79] [Symptom clusters in Long COVID in the UK: prospective community-based cohort study using unsupervised machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05213)
*Jasmine Aherne,Ines Henriques-Cadby,Thomas House*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究利用英国国家统计局COVID-19感染调查数据（n=207,319），通过Jaccard症状距离矩阵结合热图、非度量多维尺度和凝聚层次聚类三种方法，分析长期COVID的症状共现模式。研究发现随着发病时间的延长，症状共现率增加，器官系统间的症状可分离性减弱，并识别出三组症状群及三种早期长期COVID表型，其中气促、乏力/疲倦和肌肉酸痛为核心症状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期COVID的症状多样且广泛，对医疗系统、经济和个人造成重大负担。通过追踪症状共现可以捕捉患者体验，改善对该疾病的理解、诊断和管理。

Method: 使用ONS COVID-19感染调查（CIS）中23种长期COVID症状的二元调查数据，构建Jaccard症状间距离矩阵，采用三种可视化方法（热图、非度量多维缩放、凝聚层次聚类完全连接法）分析症状共现，分别对全部响应（n=207,319）和按发病后时间分层至24个月的响应（n=30,224零月）进行分析。

Result: 长期COVID持续时间越长，症状共现越显著。识别出神经/系统症状、胃肠症状和呼吸症状三类症状群，但随发病时间延长，按器官系统的共现可分离性减弱。气促、乏力/疲倦和肌肉酸痛为核心症状。发现三种可能早期长期COVID表型。

Conclusion: 长期COVID的症状体验从早期到晚期的演化特征表现为症状负担增加及多系统表现。症状共现分析为长期COVID的疾病演化和潜在表型识别提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Long COVID is a condition usually defined by persisting symptoms following infection by the SARS-CoV-2 virus beyond the acute phase of infection. The condition has a significant impact on healthcare systems, the economy, and the individuals living with it. Due to the diverse and extensive symptomatology of long COVID, symptom co-occurrence tracking can be used to capture patient experiences and improve understanding, diagnosis, and management. Here, we leverage the UK Office for National Statistics (ONS) COVID-19 Infection Survey (CIS). The CIS was run between April 2020 and March 2023. On February 3, 2021, the ONS launched a new CIS question evaluating self-reported symptom persistence of 23 long COVID symptoms post self-reported COVID-19 infection. We use Jaccard symptom-by symptom distance matrices, derived from the binary survey responses of the presence of each symptom. Three methods are then used to visualise symptom co-occurrence: heatmaps, non-metric multidimensional scaling, and agglomerative hierarchical clustering with complete linkage. We split our analysis into two parts, first looking at all long COVID survey responses (n = 207,319) and then looking at responses stratified by time-since-onset of long COVID up to 24 months (n = 30,224 at zero months-since-onset). We find higher symptom co-occurrence in prolonged long COVID. We also find clusters of neurological/systemic symptoms, gastrointestinal symptoms, and respiratory symptoms, with separability in symptom co-occurrence by organ system becoming less pronounced as time-since-onset of long COVID increases. Shortness of breath, weakness/tiredness, and muscle ache present as core symptoms long COVID. We demonstrate that the symptom experience of long COVID evolves from early stages to late stages with higher symptom burden and multi-systemic presentation. We also find three possible phenotypes of early long COVID.

</details>

### [80] [Exact quantum spin liquids with topological order on maple-leaf and trellis lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04319)
*Li Ern Chern,Roderich Moessner,Claudio Castelnovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在penta-coordinated的枫叶和格子（trellis）晶格上构造了具有依赖于键的各向异性相互作用的自旋模型，产生了与Kitaev蜂窝模型类似的精确Z₂量子自旋液体，并对其基态通量、Chern数和拓扑激发进行了系统表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Kitaev模型的精确可解量子自旋液体框架到更多晶格几何（特别是具有五配位结构的枫叶和trellis晶格），并理解拓扑序、通量序和任意子激发在这些系统中的行为。

Method: 构造带有键依赖各向异性相互作用的精确可解自旋模型；利用流式（flux）扇区、Chern数和拓扑激发描述基态；采用交换副本量子蒙特卡洛（replica exchange QMC）模拟研究低温下规范通量的有序性；通过奇偶长度基本plaquette的映射建立涡旋与两类任意子的对应关系，并推导Majorana费米子质量项的解析表达式。

Result: 发现低温下规范通量有序化：每个单元三角形具有±π/2通量且符号在系统中均匀一致，每个单元六边形（正方形）具有0通量（π通量）。绘制了两个模型的拓扑相图，揭示了Z₂和Ising拓扑序的多种参数区域。在二聚体极限下建立了单个涡旋（通量激发）与两类任意子之间的对应关系，并发现枫叶模型的两个二聚体极限在无涡旋扇区中可以平滑相连，而在某些双涡旋扇区中则被费米子间隙闭合转变所隔开。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了在五配位晶格上可以实现精确可解的Z₂量子自旋液体，并揭示了不同拓扑相之间通过费米子质量项消失所标记的相变机制，以及在奇长度plaquette系统中利用二聚体极限绕过简并微扰理论障碍的有效方法。

Abstract: We construct spin models with bond-dependent anisotropic interactions on the penta-coordinated maple-leaf and trellis lattices, which yield exact $\mathbb{Z}_2$ quantum spin liquids akin to the Kitaev honeycomb model. We characterize the resulting ground states by their flux sectors, Chern numbers, and topological excitations. Using replica exchange quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we find that the gauge fluxes are ordered at sufficiently low temperatures such that every unit triangle has $\pm π/2$-flux, where the sign is uniform across the system, and every unit hexagon (square) has $0$-flux ($π$-flux) within the parameter space of interest. We map out the topological phase diagram for each model, which reveals parameter regimes hosting $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and Ising topological orders, and we derive an analytical expression for the mass term of the Majorana fermions, the vanishing of which indicates a transition between these phases. We further establish the correspondence between individual vortices (i.e., flux excitations) and two species of anyons in the dimer limit, overcoming the obstacle faced by degenerate perturbation theory in treating odd-length elementary plaquettes. Interestingly, we find that two dimer limits of the maple-leaf model with distinct assignments of anyon species can be smoothly connected to each other in the vortex-free sector, but they are separated by fermion-gap-closing transitions in certain two-vortex sectors.

</details>


### [81] [Dual Charge-Density-Waves in Two-dimensional DyTe3 and Their Distinct Impacts on Magneto-Transport Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04370)
*Shuvankar Gupta,Yasemin Ozbek,Olajumoke Oluwatobiloba Emmanuel,Maya Bostock,Johnathan Kowalski,Pengpeng Zhang,Xianglin Ke*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了DyTe3在低温下两个正交电荷密度波（CDW1和CDW2）共存对其电子输运性质的影响：CDW2的出现显著增强了磁电阻并导致多能带控制的非线性霍尔响应，而CDW1转变未见明显的磁输运异常，表明两者在本质和输运影响上截然不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管电荷密度波（CDW）是宏观量子态且已被广泛研究，但在CDW存在下电磁输运性质的演化仍未被充分探索，特别是多个CDW共存体系中的输运后果需要深入理解。

Method: 通过低温电子输运测量，系统研究DyTe3中两个正交方向共存CDW（CDW1和CDW2）对磁电阻和霍尔响应的影响，并分析其随温度及磁场的变化行为。

Despite significant research efforts, the evolution of electromagnetic transport properties in the presence of CDWs remains largely unexplored. Here, we report the effects of two orthogonal CDWs (CDW1 and CDW2) coexisting at low-temperature in DyTe3 on its electronic transport properties. Importantly, we find that while no clear magnetotransport anomaly is resolved across CDW1 transition under the present experimental conditions, the emergence of CDW2 drives a drastic enhancement in magnetoresistance and a multiband-governed nonlinear Hall response. These results demonstrate that CDW1 and CDW2 in DyTe3 are fundamentally different and impact its electronic transport in qualitatively distinct ways, establishing DyTe3 as a model system for understanding the transport consequences of multiple CDWs in quasi-two-dimensional materials.

Abstract: The discovery of superconductivity in Ruddlesden-Popper nickelates has raised a central question: how does layer architecture shape the electronic, magnetic, and lattice interactions relevant to pairing? Here, we report a detailed comparative study of the two polymorphs of La3Ni2O7--the alternating monolayer-trilayer (LNO-1313) and bilayer (LNO-2222) structures--and the related trilayer compound La4Ni3O10, using both Ni L3- and O K-edge RIXS. Compared to LNO-2222, LNO-1313 and La4Ni3O10 have weaker orbital polarization, enhanced 3d8L character, a reduced out-of-plane magnetic-exchange scale, and stronger EPC. Within an effective local-moment framework, an entangled-dimer scenario provides a natural description of the spin excitations generated by strong antiferromagnetic interlayer coupling. Its advantage over conventional spin-wave theory is clearest in bilayer LNO-2222, where the interlayer coupling dominates the intralayer interactions. These findings provide critical experimental constraints for future theoretical models for the low-energy physics relevant to superconductivity in these layered nickelates.

Abstract: We study steady-state transport in a one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard chain coupled to particle reservoirs at the boundaries and to local dephasing baths at each site, using the time-evolving block decimation (TEBD) method to solve the Lindblad master equation. In the absence of dephasing, the current exhibits two well-separated maxima as a function of the boundary driving rate, reflecting the distinct charge and spin energy scales of the strongly correlated regime. Upon introducing dephasing, we find two distinct dephasing-induced transport-enhancement regimes, in contrast to the single enhancement previously reported for spinless fermions. Analysis of the non-equilibrium steady state in the Hamiltonian eigenbasis reveals that the two regimes originate from distinct dephasing-induced redistribution processes: the first involves redistribution within the uppermost Hubbard band, while the second involves transitions between Hubbard bands. Our results demonstrate how many-body correlations shape the interplay between coherent driving, dephasing, and quantum Zeno physics in strongly correlated open systems.

Abstract: We present a systematic numerical study of the spin-1 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice in an out-of-plane magnetic field, using Density Matrix Renormalization Group (DMRG) methods. By mapping out the quantum phase diagram as a function of the single-ion anisotropy $D_z$ and magnetic field, we identify distinct dipolar and quadrupolar spin supersolid states, characterized by spontaneous U(1) symmetry breaking with finite spin superfluid stiffness coexisting with longitudinal translational symmetry breaking. At zero field, the dipolar spin supersolid with a 'Y'-type spin configuration persists down to $D_z = 0$, whereas the quadrupolar spin supersolid prevails at large $D_z$. At intermediate fields, the phase diagram is dominated by an up-up-down phase. At high fields below saturation, a quadrupolar spin superfluid emerges in the large-$D_z$ regime, whereas a dipolar spin supersolid with a 'V'-type spin configuration dominates at small $D_z$. These phases are characterized through their order parameters and spin superfluid stiffness using calculations on various system sizes. Furthermore, the dynamical spin structure factor is obtained across the phase diagram, where characteristic spectral signatures of different phases are observed, including the gapless Goldstone mode and the roton-like minima. These features are directly accessible to inelastic neutron scattering experiments. Our results provide a theoretical understanding of the interplay between frustrations, anisotropy, and Zeeman interactions in driving distinct spin supersolid phases in the spin-1 system, which are relevant to various triangular-lattice antiferromagnets such as Na$_2$BaNi(PO$_4$)$_2$ and K$_2$Ni(SeO$_3$)$_2$.

Method: 实验上测量Ru₂Mn₀.₅Ti₀.₅Ge的反常霍尔效应，并结合第一性原理（ab initio）计算，分析Kondo杂化诱导的平带与自旋轨道耦合反交叉如何产生Berry曲率。

Abstract: The confluence of strong electronic correlations and Berry curvature-driven transport constitutes a largely underexplored frontier in quantum materials research, particularly in systems where electronic correlations arise from flat-band physics rather than conventional $f$-electron states. Here, we report an intrinsic Berry curvature driven anomalous Hall transport in the dilute Kondo system Ru$_2$Mn$_{0.5}$Ti$_{0.5}$Ge, a non-$f$-electron Heusler alloy hosting flat bands with van Hove singularity proximate to the Fermi level. The anomalous Hall response is strikingly non-monotonic, violating the Fermi-liquid scaling relation near the magnetic transition and is restored only at low temperature, coincident with the onset of Kondo coherence. \textit{Ab initio} calculations strongly establish that the Berry curvature originates from the Kondo hybridization induced interplay of flat bands and spin-orbit coupling mediated anticrossings at the Fermi level. Our findings demonstrate that Kondo coherence of flat bands bears a profound impact on Berry curvature associated ferroic responses, mandating a rigorous theoretical understanding of anomalous transport in the regime where reciprocal space topology and strong correlations are intrinsically intertwined.

Abstract: A search is presented for the rare, loop-induced Higgs boson decay H $\to$ Z$γ$, where Z $\to$ $\ell^+\ell^-$ and $\ell$ = e or $μ$. The search is performed using a sample of proton-proton (pp) collision data at the center-of-mass energies of 13 and 13.6 TeV, recorded by the CMS experiment at the LHC and corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 200 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis design separately considers and optimizes sensitivity to Higgs boson production via gluon-gluon fusion, vector boson fusion, and associated-production processes. The signal is extracted from a simultaneous fit to the invariant mass distributions of the $\ell^+\ell^-γ$ system in the various production channels and event categories. The observed (expected) signal strength $μ$, defined as the ratio of the measured product the of cross section and decay branching fraction value $σ$(pp $\to$ H)$\mathcal{B}$(H $\to$ Z$γ$) to the corresponding value predicted in the standard model, is found to be $μ$ = 1.10$^{+0.52}_{-0.61}$ (1.00$^{+0.49}_{-0.46}$) for a Higgs boson mass of 125.38 GeV. The signal has an observed (expected) significance of 1.9 (2.3) standard deviations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解矩阵方程 X^{-1}-A=0 的迭代方法（如Hyperpower方法）在收敛阶与计算成本之间存在权衡，提高收敛阶通常需要更多矩阵-矩阵乘积。作者旨在找到一种方法，在保持计算成本不变的基础上提高收敛阶，从而提升求解效率。

Method: 该方法首次引入标量范数型加速器（scalar norm-type accelerators），将其应用于已有的迭代格式中，将收敛阶从四阶升级到六阶，但其矩阵-矩阵乘法的计算成本与四阶Hyperpower方法相当。论文证明了方法的收敛性、收敛阶、效率及稳定性。

Result: 该方案实现了50%的收敛阶增长（从四阶到六阶），同时不增加矩阵-矩阵乘积数量。在大规模矩阵（元素数达10^6）上，该方法的数值结果优于现有的四阶、六阶和八阶方法。此外，在数字图像恢复应用中展示了良好的表现。

Conclusion: 该技术开启了一种新型的、具有良好性能和可扩展性的迭代方法设计思路，为求解矩阵方程提供了更高效的工具，并在图像处理等实际应用中展现出实用价值。

Abstract: In this manuscript, scalar norm-type accelerators are used for first time in order to increase the order of convergence of known iterative methods for solving the matrix equation $X^{-1}-A=0$, while decreasing the number of matrix-matrix products. Specifically, our proposed scheme upgrades the order of convergence in a $50\%$, achieving the sixth-order of convergence with the computational cost of fourth-order Hyperpower method, in terms of matrix-matrix multiplications. In the main results, not only the convergence and its order is proven, but also its efficiency and stability. This new technique provides good numerical results, even compared with existing fourth-, sixth- and eighth-order schemes in large matrices of up to $10^6$ entries, but also promises the opening of a new kind of procedures with good performance and scalability. Finally, an application to digital image restoration is made to show the behavior of the method, compared with existing ones.

Abstract: I present a matrix-free symmetric interior penalty discontinuous Galerkin method for adaptively refined Cartesian meshes on GPUs. I prove that this construction is equivalent to the standard non-matching formulation. ### [89] [A Complete Resolution of Forsythe's Conjecture for Restarted Conjugate Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04659)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 给出了按重启长度的完整分类：s=1,2,3时Forsythe猜想成立——每个问题要么终止，要么偶数/奇数重启子序列的残差方向均收敛；s≥4时猜想不成立——存在维度s+4的对角正定问题永不终止且偶子序列残差方向不收敛。同时识别出一个尖锐阈值s*=4。

Conclusion: Forsythe猜想被完整解决：其普适性结论恰好在s∈{1,2,3}时成立，在s≥4时被推翻。这是对该六十年未解问题的最终、完整的分类结果，核心贡献包括尖锐阈值的识别及从s=4到所有更大重启长度的反例构造方法。

Abstract: Forsythe's conjecture, published in 1968, asserts that for each restart length $s$, every exact-arithmetic restarted conjugate-gradient iteration on a real symmetric positive definite problem either terminates or has normalised residuals that converge separately along the even and odd restart subsequences. Apart from the classical steepest-descent case, this asymptotic question remained unresolved in full generality for nearly six decades. We give a complete classification by restart length in the original finite-dimensional setting and identify a sharp threshold. For $s=2$ and $s=3$, every problem either terminates or has convergent even and odd residual directions. For every $s\ge4$, there is a diagonal positive definite counterexample of dimension $s+4$ which never terminates and whose even residual directions do not converge. Together with Akaike's theorem for $s=1$, this shows that the conjectured universal conclusion is true precisely for $s\in\{1,2,3\}$ and false for every $s\ge4$. The positive results follow from a degree-independent double-orthogonality identity and an analysis of the low-degree fixed-point sets. At restart length four, rational interval arithmetic and Sturm sequences certify a transverse Hopf point of the leading vector field of the rescaled squared-weight map. Analytic periodic-orbit and shadowing arguments yield the counterexample at restart length four, and degree elevation extends the construction to every larger restart length.

</details>

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for deriving semi-discrete discontinuous-Galerkin (DG) methods using operator semigroups for scalar conservation laws, then apply it to construct a class of high-order OFDG-type schemes [13] satisfying infinitely many local entropy inequalities with general E-fluxes on non-uniform meshes. Finally, we prove optimal error estimates for smooth solutions to nonlinear scalar conservation laws, and prove strong convergence for discontinuous solutions to strictly convex conservation laws via compensated compactness.

### [91] [Energy-Consistent Splitting and Decomposition Approaches for Coupled port-Hamiltonian ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04884)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂是数值积分（耦合）port-Hamiltonian系统的一种有吸引力的方法，因为它允许在单个子问题层面利用系统底层结构。然而，分解的选择并不唯一，且可能强烈影响计算效率和对原系统能量行为的保持。需要一个系统的框架来指导分解策略的选择。

Abstract: Operator splitting provides an attractive approach for the numerical integration of (coupled) port-Hamiltonian systems, as it allows the underlying system structure to be exploited at the level of the individual subproblems. However, the choice of the decomposition is not unique and may strongly affect both the computational efficiency and the preservation of the energy behavior of the original system. In particular, we compare decompositions that preserve the structure with approaches that exploit lower-dimensional subsystem dynamics or separated time scales. A scalable electro-thermal benchmark with fast electrical and slow thermal dynamics is employed to assess accuracy, energy behavior, and computational efficiency. The results demonstrate that preserving the port-Hamiltonian structure of the subflows is essential for energy-consistent splitting, whereas decompositions that exploit subsystem structure or time-scale separation can provide substantial computational advantages. In particular, the time-scale decomposition yields significant efficiency gains for systems with pronounced multirate characteristics, while structure-destroying decompositions may lead to undesirable energy behavior.

Abstract: We present a unifying framework to construct local Fortin operators for conforming mixed finite element pairs for the Stokes equations. The operators are constructed to satisfy the divergence-preservation property, local stability and approximation properties, and certain trace-preservation properties. For the latter, some of the finite element pairs require an enrichment of the velocity space. We present the construction for the $P_d-P_0$ element, the Bernardi-Raugel element, the conforming Crouzeix-Raviart element, a modified MINI element and generalised Taylor-Hood elements. Furthermore, we discuss implications of the existence of such a Fortin operator beyond inf-sup stability. These include applications to non-Newtonian fluid flow, problems with inhomogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, as well as uniform inf-sup stability relevant for domain approximation and moving domains.

Abstract: We analyze the numerical approximation of time-harmonic scattering by highly heterogeneous penetrable obstacles. Here, we further consider the situation where the scatterer contains different materials, with a characteristic size $\varepsilon$ such that $k\varepsilon \ll 1$. We propose a high-order multiscale finite element method, and provide an error analysis that is explicit in both $k$ and $\varepsilon$. Crucially, our error estimates suggest that using a high-order method should reduce the computational cost for large frequencies, which is corroborated by numerical examples.

Abstract: We consider (feed-forward) Neural Networks (NNs) for the emulation of the solution to singularly perturbed second order boundary value problems, with two small parameters. Abstract: We develop an energy-optimal generalized scalar auxiliary variable (EOP-GSAV) framework for nonlinear index-one port-Hamiltonian differential-algebraic equations (pH-DAEs). Exploiting the port-Hamiltonian structure, we separate the nonlinear effort, interconnection, and dissipation terms from a constant implicit core. The resulting BDF-1 and BDF-2 schemes require one linear solve per time step with a reusable factorization, while retaining discrete passivity and accurate tracking of the Hamiltonian. The schemes are compared with the implicit midpoint method equipped with full, modified, and frozen-Jacobian Newton iterations. Numerical experiments ranging from a strongly state-dependent nonlinear stress test to large-scale benchmarks demonstrate robust and competitive performance, with substantial efficiency gains in matched-accuracy regimes. A comparison with SUNDIALS IDA shows comparable work-precision behavior at equal order despite a non-specialized Python/SciPy implementation, while unrestricted variable-order adaptive IDA is faster in the high-accuracy regime.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统地理解不同激活函数如何影响浅层神经网络对高维混合光滑函数的逼近能力，特别是希望将逼近速率与目标函数的混合光滑度以及激活函数的性质联系起来，从而给出最优的理论刻画。

Method: 首先建立激活无关的Fourier-block原理，将全局逼近率化为对激活函数的单变量逼近阶ρ和混合光滑度α的组合min{α,ρ}；随后引入一个结构化的单变量逼近条件来证明Fourier-block性质并给出显式参数。对于ReLU^k，通过构造匹配的代数下界来证明最优化。

Result: 得到了匹配的代数逼近指数：ReLU^k在任意维度的最优指数为min{α,k+1}（对数因子意义下精确）；该框架同样对基数B样条和soft-ReLU^k给出指数min{α,k+1}，而对ELU和cosine激活给出完整的混合光滑指数α，上界均精确到对数因子。

Conclusion: 本文给出了浅层神经网络对混合Sobolev函数逼近的系统性刻画，揭示了激活函数的单变量逼近阶如何决定全局多维逼近速率，并为ReLU^k等常用激活建立了理论上最优的下界结果。

Abstract: We investigate the best $L_2$ approximation of mixed Sobolev spaces by shallow neural networks with $n$ neurons and general activation functions. We first establish an activation-independent Fourier-block principle: if an activation has univariate approximation order $ρ$ in the sense of the Fourier-block property, then the global approximation rate has algebraic order $\min\{α,ρ\}$ for target functions of mixed smoothness $α$, up to explicit logarithmic factors. To verify this property for concrete activations, we introduce a structured univariate approximation condition that implies the Fourier-block property with explicit parameters. For $\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, a matching algebraic lower bound identifies $\min\{α,k+1\}$ as the optimal algebraic approximation exponent in any dimension, up to logarithmic factors in the upper bound. The framework also yields the exponent $\min\{α,k+1\}$ for cardinal B-splines and soft-$\mathrm{ReLU}^k$, and the full mixed-smoothness exponent $α$ for ELU and cosine activations, again up to logarithmic~factors.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Approximating CDTW Distance of Piecewise Algebraic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04294)
*Alperen A. Ergür,Shamik Khowala*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续动态时间规整（CDTW）距离度量，提出了首个支持分段高次代数曲线（在欧几里得范数下）的乘法近似算法，并给出了多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）的复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线数据在金融、地震学、医学等领域广泛存在，常用相似度度量（DTW和Fréchet距离）分别对采样率和异常值敏感，鲁棒性不足。CDTW是更稳健的替代度量，但现有精确算法仅适用于分段线性曲线，且已有近似算法也局限于分段线性曲线，缺乏对更一般（高次）分段代数曲线的处理方案。

Method: 提出一种针对欧几里得范数下、分段（较高次）代数曲线之间CDTW距离的近似算法，实现了乘法误差为ε的完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS），复杂度为O((m+n)^{19/6}(1/ε)^{10/3} log((m+n)/ε^2))，其中m和n为两条输入曲线的分段数量。

Result: 作者给出了复杂度为 O((m+n)^{19/6}(1/ε)^{10/3} log((m+n)/ε^2)) 的FPTAS算法，首次将CDTW近似计算推广到分段高次代数曲线，显著拓展了现有方法的适用范围。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了CDTW距离在非线性（高次）分段曲线上的计算空白，为实际应用中更复杂曲线数据的相似性度量提供了高效可用的近似算法。

Abstract: Curves as input data naturally arise in a variety of fields including finance, seismology, medicine, spatio-temporal data mining, malicious activity detection, and more. A common way to analyze these data sets is to do similarity matching or clustering. The most common metrics used for measuring similarity of curves are Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) and Fréchet distance. These metrics are sensitive to sampling rate and outliers respectively, and do not yield robust outcomes. Continuous Dynamic Time Warping (CDTW) is a more robust distance metric that improves upon DTW and Fréchet distances. Existing algorithms for CDTW are either exact algorithms that focus on non-Euclidean norms and piecewise linear curves, or approximation algorithms limited to piecewise linear curves. We present an approximation algorithm for computing the CDTW distance under Euclidean norm between piecewise (higher degree) algebraic curves. That is, we present a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme (FPTAS) of multiplicative error $\varepsilon$, with $O \left( (m+n)^{\frac{19}{6}} (\frac{1}{\varepsilon})^{\frac{10}{3}} \log \left( \frac{ (m+n) }{\varepsilon^2} \right) \right)$ complexity, where $m$ and $n$ are the number of pieces of the two input curves.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Schatten norms and determinants of linear combinations of matrix tensor powers via virtual representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04341)
*Martin Áron Juhász,Mihály Weiner*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于表示论的精确方法，用于在固定维度d和项数s下，以多项式时间计算X_n=Σ t_i A_i^{⊗n}的行列式及Schatten p-范数（0<p<∞）。该方法利用Schur-Weyl对偶实现同时块分解，并结合Jacobi-Trudi恒等式将Schur模替换为对称幂的张量积的有符号组合。对于d=3情形，每个不可约贡献可化为两个对称幂项之差，已实现开源代码，经n=18的三项3×3迹范数基准测试仅需约47秒，计算精度达相对误差3.4×10^-15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当矩阵X_n为多个张量幂的线性组合时，直接计算其行列式或Schatten范数的复杂度随n呈指数增长。尽管单张量幂情形是初等的，且两项组合的行列式可化简为多项式个标量因子，但对于三项及以上组合不存在类似的初等化简方法，亟需一种能高效计算的系统性方案。

Method: 利用Schur-Weyl对偶对X_n进行同时块分解；在Grothendieck环中通过Jacobi-Trudi恒等式将Schur模表示为对称幂张量积的有符号线性组合，从而将每个不可约块的计算化为可显式计算且规模可控的张量积行列式/范数问题。对于d=3，每个不可约贡献进一步化简为两个对称幂项之差，并给出开源实现。

Result: 在固定d和s下，算法在n上为多项式时间。d=3的基准测试中，一个真实的3×3三项迹范数问题（n=18）在单线程CPU上约47秒完成，而直接存储未化简矩阵需约2.4×10^18字节。在公共范围n≤9内，直接计算与化简计算结果的相对误差低于3.4×10^-15。

Conclusion: 本文给出了张量幂线性组合（三项及以上）行列式与Schatten范数的首个精确多项式时间算法，填补了此前缺乏初等化简方法的空白；理论框架具有一般性，d=3情形的开源实现验证了方法的有效性与数值精度。

Abstract: Let $$X_n=\sum_{i=1}^s t_i A_i^{\otimes n},$$ where $A_1,\ldots,A_s\in M_d(\mathbb C)$ and $t_1,\ldots,t_s\in\mathbb C$ are fixed, while $n$ grows. Direct computation of determinants or Schatten norms of $X_n$ is exponential in $n$. For a single tensor power these quantities are elementary, and even the determinant of a generic two-term combination admits a reduction to polynomially many scalar factors; however, no analogous elementary reduction is available for three or more terms.
  We give an exact representation-theoretic method which, for fixed $d$ and $s$, computes $\|X_n\|_p$, $0<p<\infty$, and determinants in polynomial time in $n$. Schur--Weyl duality yields a simultaneous block decomposition, while Jacobi--Trudi identities in the Grothendieck ring replace Schur modules by signed combinations of tensor products of symmetric powers.
  For $d=3$, each irreducible contribution reduces to the difference of two explicitly computable symmetric-power terms, leading to an open-source implementation. In a single-thread CPU benchmark, a genuine three-term $3\times3$ trace-norm problem with $n=18$ is evaluated in about $47$ seconds, whereas just storing the unreduced matrix would require approximately $2.4\times10^{18}$ bytes. Direct and reduced computations agree to relative error below $3.4\times10^{-15}$ throughout their common range $n\leq9$.

</details>


### [99] [On a basepoint issue in the tempered dual of $p$-adic $SL(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05055)
*Marie Dautheville*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究非阿基米德局部域 F 上 SL(n,F) 的 Levi 子群 M 的离散序列表示在幺正非分歧特征标扭转作用下的轨道问题。作者针对 n≥8 且非素数、非 9 的整数，构造了无不动点的轨道族，并证明了在素数或小维数情形下 W_𝒪 的作用总有不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究属于 p进群表示论中离散序列表示在扭转作用下的轨道理论。理解全局稳定化子 W_𝒪 在轨道上的作用是否有不动点，对研究离散序列表示的首次参数化、内窥性以及有关 L-封包和加强参数的性质具有重要意义。此前对这种不动点问题的系统性研究不足，本文旨在为一般维数提供构造性和排除性结论。

Method: 对于可构造无不动点轨道的情形，选取除数 m|n（m≥2 且 m|q-1），构造 Levi 子群 M 包含 m 块大小为 k≥2 和 2 块大小为 m 的块结构。核心方法是构造 GL(m,F) 和 GL(k,F) 上的超尖（supercuspidal）表示，使其满足特定条件，进而构建所需的轨道族 𝒪 并分析 W_𝒪 的作用。对于被排除的维数（素数或小整数 n），则直接证明任意轨道 𝒪 上 W_𝒪 总有不动点。

Result: 本文对每个满足条件的除数 m，成功构造了无不动点的轨道族。同时证明了当 n 为素数或小维数（如 n<8 或 n=9）时，所有轨道的全局稳定化子作用均存在不动点。

Conclusion: 作者证明了 SL(n,F) 的 Levi 子群的离散序列在扭转作用下的固定点问题取决于维数 n 的算术结构：素数或小维数情形保证存在不动点，而合数 n（n≥8, n≠9）通过选择适当的除数 m 可以构造出无不动点的轨道。该结果为理解 p 进群表示论中这一类固定点问题提供了完整的维数分类刻画。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a non-archimedean local field of characteristic zero. Let $M$ be a Levi subgroup of $G = \mathrm{SL}(n,F)$. Consider the action on the discrete series of $M$ of the group of unitary unramified characters of $M$ (where a character acts by twisting). Given an orbit $\mathcal{O}$ for that action, let $W_\mathcal{O}$ be the global stabilizer of $\mathcal{O}$ in the Weyl group of $M$. We construct families of orbits $\mathcal{O}$ for which the action of $W_\mathcal{O}$ has no fixed point.
  More precisely, let $n \geq 8$ be an integer that is not prime and not equal to $9$. We attach families of orbits without fixed points to any divisor $m \geq 2$ of $n$ such that $m \;| \; q-1$ where $q$ is the order of the residue field of $F$. The Levi subgroup $M \subset G$ for the corresponding families has $m$ blocks of size $k\geq 2$ and $2$ blocks of size $m$. The core of the paper is the construction of superculpidal representations of $\mathrm{GL}(m,F)$ and $\mathrm{GL}(k,F)$ satisfying some conditions that we require to construct the families of orbits $\mathcal{O}$ that we expects.
  In addition, we study the fixed point problem for the dimensions $n$ that we excluded above, i.e., prime or small dimensions. We prove that there is always a fixed point under the action of $W_\mathcal{O}$ in those cases.

</details>

### [100] [Nebular Spectra of the Extremely Metal-Poor SN II 2023ufx Over a Year After Explosion: A Massive Progenitor and Unique Circumstellar Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04307)
*Evan Jennerjahn,Michael A. Tucker,Christopher S. Kochanek,Luc Dessart,Willem B. Hoogendam,Benjamin J. Shappee*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了贫金属(<0.1 Z☉)II型超新星2023ufx及其宿主星系的观测结果。爆发约一年后的深场星云光谱显示出三重峰[O I]发射线轮廓，暗示爆发不对称；通过与星云光谱模型比较推断其零龄主序质量为25-35 M☉，且[Ca II]/[O I]发射线比≈0.4支持这一结论。晚期(约2年)光度资料中逐渐减弱且宽箱状的Hα发射以及光变曲线变平表明爆发前数百年至数千年存在复杂的质量损失历史。宿主星系光学和近红外成像证实其为矮星系，恒星质量10^6.6±0.1 M☉，恒星形成率10^-2.5±0.1 M☉/yr。SED拟合与质量-金属丰度关系均得出环境金属丰度为0.02-0.07 Z☉。这些观测共同确认SN 2023ufx是一颗极贫金属、强剥离的红超巨星爆炸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极贫金属环境下的II型超新星及其前身星性质，特别是验证低金属丰度环境下红超巨星的质量损失历史与爆炸不对称性是否与近太阳金属丰度环境不同，为恒星演化与超新星爆发机制提供关键观测约束。

Method: 利用深场星云光谱(约爆发后一年)对[O I]发射线及其三重峰轮廓进行建模分析；通过比较星云光谱模型反推前身星零龄主序质量；结合[Ca II]/[O I]发射线比进行交叉验证；对晚期(约2年)光学光度数据中的Hα发射轮廓和光变曲线进行形态学与时序分析；利用宿主星系的光学和近红外成像数据进行SED拟合和恒星质量-金属丰度标度关系推算，获得宿主星系的环境金属丰度、恒星质量和恒星形成率。

Result: SN 2023ufx的前身星零龄主序质量为25-35 M☉，[Ca II]/[O I]比≈0.4；宿主星系为恒星质量10^6.6±0.1 M☉、恒星形成率10^-2.5±0.1 M☉/yr的矮星系，环境金属丰度0.02-0.07 Z☉；晚期Hα发射减弱和光变曲线变平表明存在复杂的前期质量损失历史；综合确认该超新星是极贫金属、强剥离红超巨星的爆炸，且爆炸具有不对称性。

Conclusion: SN 2023ufx是迄今探测到的极贫金属环境中一颗大质量(25-35 M☉)红超巨星爆炸的典型代表，其不对称爆炸三元组[O I]发射线特征与复杂的晚期质量历史为理解极低金属丰度下的恒星演化终止阶段、质量损失机制以及核心坍缩超新星的爆炸几何结构提供了重要观测样本，并对矮星系的恒星形成与化学增丰有参考意义。

Abstract: We present new observations of the metal-poor ($< 0.1$ $\rm Z_\odot$) Type II supernova 2023ufx and its host galaxy. The deep nebular spectrum, obtained roughly a year after explosion, has a triple-peaked [O I] emission profile suggesting an asymmetric explosion. Comparison to nebular spectra models suggests a zero-age main sequence mass of $ M_{\rm ZAMS} \sim 25-35$ $\rm M_\odot$, which is supported by the low [Ca II]/[O I] emission-line ratio of $\approx0.4$. The diminishing, broad, boxy H$α$ emission and flattening of the light curve in the late ($\sim2$ yr) photometry suggest a complex mass-loss history in the centuries to millennia before explosion. New optical and near-infrared imaging of the host galaxy confirms that it is a dwarf, with a stellar mass of $10^{6.6\pm0.1}$ $\rm M_\odot$ and a SFR of $10^{-2.5\pm0.1}$ $\rm M_\odot$/yr. Results from both SED fitting and galaxy stellar mass-metallicity scaling relations all lead to an environmental metallicity estimate of $0.02-0.07$ $\rm Z_\odot$. Taken together, these observations confirm that SN 2023ufx is the explosion of a very metal-poor, heavily stripped red supergiant.

</details>


### [101] [A Differentiable Neural Surrogate for Photon Propagation in Neutrino Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04695)
*Felix J. Yu,Berthy T. Feng,Nicholas Kamp,Carlos A. Argüelles*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出candela，一个基于可微分SIREN神经场的方法，用于学习IceCube中微子望远镜中光子传播的Green函数。它将带电粒子能量沉积分解为点源并叠加其传感器响应，实现事件模拟比现有方法快50-100倍，同时保持产量预测误差在2%以内、时序分布在统计精度水平，并为介质散射特性优化提供端到端梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大体积中微子望远镜依赖切伦科夫光推断中微子性质，但通过高度散射的冰或水中模拟数十亿光子的传播在计算上极为昂贵，成为制约大规模事件模拟效率的主要瓶颈。

Method: 引入candela——一个可微分的SIREN（正弦表示）神经场，学习IceCube探测器（嵌入南极冰川冰中的立方千米探测器）的光子Green函数。给定点状能量沉积与传感器位置，模型预测期望光子产量及完整的到达时间分布。完整事件通过将带电粒子的能量沉积分解为点源并叠加其预测的传感器响应来模拟。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛模拟上训练后，candela生成事件的速度比现有方法快50-100倍，计算成本仅微弱地依赖中微子能量。其产量中位数与MC期望保持在2%以内，时序分布在跨越六个光子计数数量级的范围内达到MC的统计下限。

Conclusion: candela还提供关于事件参数的端到端梯度，为优化散射介质属性（通常是中微子望远镜中占主导的系统不确定性来源）开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: Large-volume neutrino telescopes infer neutrino properties from Cherenkov light, but simulating the transport of billions of photons through highly scattering ice or water is computationally costly. We introduce candela, a differentiable SIREN neural field that learns the photon Green's function of the IceCube Neutrino Observatory, a cubic-kilometer detector embedded in Antarctic glacial ice. Given a point-like energy deposit and sensor, it predicts the expected photon yield and full arrival-time distribution at the sensor. Complete events are simulated by decomposing charged-particle energy deposits into point-like sources and superposing their predicted sensor responses. Trained on Monte-Carlo simulations, candela generates events $50$--$100\times$ faster than existing methods, with cost scaling only weakly with neutrino energy. It keeps median yields within $2\%$ of the MC expectation and timing distributions at the MC statistical floor across six photon-count decades. The model also provides end-to-end gradients with respect to event parameters and opens a path toward optimizing scattering-medium properties, which often dominate systematic uncertainties in neutrino telescopes.

</details>


### [102] [A Swift X-ray view of the SMS4 sample - III: Deeper insight into previously undetected sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04310)
*Alessandro Maselli,William R. Forman,Christine Jones,Ralph P. Kraft,Matteo Perri*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文更新了Maselli等人(2024)对SMS4星表中五个明亮射电源的X射线信息，通过Swift/XRT在2026年2月前的补充观测，新探测到了四个源的X射线发射（MRC B1754-597、MRC B1817-391、MRC B1827-360和MRC B2032-350），而MRC B1814-519仅获得流量上限。基于X射线探测的位置精度约束低能波段对应体的搜寻，为一颗源提供了新的红外/光学对应体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补充并更新五个SMS4射电源的X射线观测数据，特别是eROSITA-DE DR1目录未覆盖或未探测到的源，以获得更精确的X射线位置和探测结果。

Method: 利用Swift卫星上的XRT进行了额外的X射线观测（各源约2-8 ks曝光）。对探测到的源进行延展性和硬度比分析以判断发射性质，并利用X射线位置的不确定度来约束低能波段（红外/光学）对应体的搜索与确认。

Result: 新增四个源的X射线探测：MRC B1754-597（确认并提高位置精度）、MRC B1817-391与MRC B1827-360（eROSITA未探测但Swift新检测到）、MRC B2032-350（超出DR1观测范围）。MRC B1814-519仅获上限。四颗探测源表现出软、弥散的X射线发射特征，可能来自星系群核心或热星系冕。为MRC B1817-391提供了新的红外/光学对应体，并确认了其余三颗源的已有对应体。

Conclusion: Swift补充观测有效填补了eROSITA-DE DR1在X射线覆盖和灵敏度上的空白，进一步支持这些明亮射电源与星系群核心或热星系冕环境之间的关联。

Abstract: We update the X-ray information given in Maselli et al. (2024) for five bright radio sources in the SMS4 catalog, thanks to additional observations with the X-Ray Telescope (XRT) on board the Neil Gehrels Swift Observatory (hereafter Swift) carried out through February 2026. X-ray emission from MRC B1754-597, previously based only on data from the eROSITA-DE DR1 catalogs, is now detected also by Swift, which yields a more precise positional uncertainty. Thanks to ~7 ks of additional exposure with Swift we are now able to detect MRC B1817-391, a source for which no X-ray counterpart is found in eROSITA-DE DR1. Another source that is not detected in the eROSITA-DE DR1 catalogs, and that was not observed by Swift earlier than 2025, is MRC B1827-360: with a ~6 ks X-ray observation, the source is detected. Finally, based on ~8 ks of additional exposure, we detect X-ray emission for MRC B2032-350, a source that lies out of the DR1 footprint. In contrast, additional exposure (~2 ks) just gives an upper limit on the X-ray emission of MRC B1814-519. The analysis of the extent and the hardness ratio of the four detected X-ray sources suggests the presence of soft, diffuse X-ray emission as expected from galaxy group cores or hot galaxy coronae. Using the positional uncertainty of the X-ray detections to constrain the search for counterparts at lower energies, we provide a new infrared/optical counterpart for MRC B1817-391 and confirm the counterparts previously reported in the literature for the three remaining sources.

</details>


### [103] [A Kinematic Measurement of the Effective Viscosity of the Intracluster Medium: The Cold Front of A3667 Seen by XRISM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04316)
*Yutaka Fujita,Yuki Omiya*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用XRISM/Resolve的X射线速度场测量，通过冷锋两侧粘性剪切层厚度来约束星系团内介质（ICM）的有效粘滞系数。以A3667星系团的典型冷锋为例，观测到视线速度跃变约535 km/s发生在100 kpc以内，结合Rayleigh问题的动量扩散模型，推导出冷、热两侧动力粘滞系数分别为Spitzer值的6.4倍和0.72倍，但受角分辨率限制为上限值，与成像方法得到的约束一致且相互独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对星系团内介质（ICM）有效粘滞系数的约束几乎完全依赖成像方法（X射线表面亮度涨落功率谱、冷锋处Kelvin-Helmholtz滚动形态），缺乏独立的运动学验证手段。本文旨在利用XRISM/Resolve的高分辨率光谱能力，开辟一条纯运动学的新途径来约束ICM粘滞性。

Method: 将冷锋界面视为经典Rayleigh问题——动量扩散深度d与时间T的关系满足运动学粘滞系数ν~d²/(4T)。利用XRISM的速度图测量A3667冷锋处的视线速度跃变（535 km/s）及发生区域宽度（~100 kpc），作为动量扩散深度的上限；以相互作用区尺度除以速度作为相互作用时间，进而推导动力粘滞系数并归一化到Spitzer值。

Result: A3667冷锋冷、热两侧的动力粘滞系数分别为5.2×10³和3.3×10³ g/cm/s，即Spitzer值的6.4倍和0.72倍。由于扩散深度受XRISM角分辨率限制（d≲100 kpc），而粘滞系数随深度平方变化，所得结果为上限值。该运动学约束与成像结果一致且相互独立。

Conclusion: XRISM/Resolve提供了一种与成像方法互补、独立的ICM粘滞系数约束途径。对A3667冷锋的测得结果（不高于Spitzer值数量级）支持了ICM粘滞性低于各向同性Spitzer值或接近其量级的图像，验证了运动学方法的可行性。

Abstract: Constraints on the effective viscosity of the intracluster medium (ICM) come almost exclusively from imaging: the power spectrum of X-ray surface brightness fluctuations, and the morphology of Kelvin-Helmholtz rolls at cold fronts. Both indicate a viscosity below the isotropic Spitzer value. XRISM/Resolve opens a complementary, purely kinematic route, in which the viscosity is inferred from the thickness of the viscous shear layer that develops at a sliding contact discontinuity. We apply it to the prototypical cold front of A3667. The interface is the classical Rayleigh problem, in which momentum diffuses to a depth d in a time T, giving a kinematic viscosity ν~ d^2/(4T). The XRISM velocity map shows a line-of-sight velocity jump of 535 km/s across the front, completed within the 100 kpc-wide region immediately inside it, the only region with an anomalously broad line. Hence d ~< 100 kpc. Adopting interaction times set by the sizes of the interacting regions divided by this velocity, we obtain dynamic viscosities of 5.2 x 10^3 and 3.3 x 10^3 g/cm/s for the cool and the hot side of the front, or 6.4 and 0.72 times the Spitzer value. Because d is limited by the angular resolution of XRISM, and the viscosity scales as its square, these are upper limits. The kinematic constraint is consistent with, and independent of, the imaging results.

</details>


### [104] [Preferential accretion onto eccentric and unequal binary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04491)
*Stanislav DeLaurentiis,Zoltán Haiman,Magdalena Siwek*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过80组超大质量双黑洞（SMBBH）流体动力学模拟，系统地刻画了"优先吸积"现象（次黑洞倾向于比主黑洞吸积更多物质）的行为规律，发现其与星周盘（CBD）的进动相关，并与偏心率、质量比密切相关，进而影响LISA可观测的双黑洞质量演化历史，同时预测周期性闪烁喷流可作为可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大质量双黑洞的星周盘通过引力与吸积影响双星演化，而此前报道的"优先吸积"现象（一个黑洞在数百个轨道周期内吸积超过另一个）尚未被完整描述和理解。作者旨在稳健地刻画该现象的行为规律、揭示其与星周盘结构的关系，并探讨其观测后果。

Method: 作者运行了80组超大质量双黑洞流体动力学（hydrodynamical）模拟，系统变化质量比qb和偏心率eb参数，定义了吸积率比λ(t) ≡ Ṁ2(t)/Ṁ1(t)以及质量比变化率q̇b ≡ d/dt(M2/M1)，在整个参数空间内分析优先吸积的特性及其与星周盘结构的关联。

Result: ①确认次黑洞倾向于超额吸积（λ≥1），且该偏好对低偏心率、低质量比双星最强，高偏心率下随时间变异性增强；②λ的时间变异性追踪星周盘的进动；③同一双星系统中可同时出现次爱丁顿与超爱丁顿吸积；④在高偏心率、高质量比双星中，气体驱动质量比趋向相等的进程极为缓慢，暗示某些双星可能无法在类星体30Myr寿命内达到质量比相等，这使得LISA能够约束SMBBH的吸积历史；⑤周期性闪烁喷流被认为是许多双星的潜在可观测特征。

Conclusion: 该研究系统揭示了优先吸积在不同质量比与偏心率参数下的行为边界与物理机制（关联于星周盘进动），并指出其重要的天体物理观测后果：不仅影响SMBBH朝向质量比相等的演化进程从而为LISA提供约束观测窗口，还预测闪烁喷流可作为可观测信号来探测此类系统。

Abstract: Supermassive binary black holes (SMBBHs) are expected to be surrounded by circumbinary disks (CBDs) which affect the binary through gravitational forces and accretion. It has been reported that the binary can experience ``preferential accretion'' where one black hole (BH) out-accretes the other for hundreds of orbits, but this asymmetry has yet to be fully described or understood. In this work, we utilize a suite of 80 SMBBH hydrodynamical simulations with varying mass ratios ($q_b$) and eccentricity ($e_b$) in order to robustly delineate the behavior of preferential accretion, determine its relationship to the structure of the CBD, and study its observational consequences. We characterize the accretion-rate ratio $λ(t) \equiv \dot{M}_2(t)/\dot{M}_1(t)$ and the mass-ratio rate of change $\dot{q}_b \equiv d/dt(M_2/M_1)$ across the suite. We confirm that the secondary tends to out-accrete the primary ($λ\geq 1$), and find this preference to be strongest for low-$e_b$, low-$q_b$ binaries and increasingly time-variable toward high $e_b$. We also find that (i) the time-variability of $λ$ tracks the precession of the CBD, (ii) there can be sub- and super-Eddington accretion in a single binary, and (iii) the gas-driven approach toward equal mass becomes particularly slow for highly eccentric, high $q_b$ binaries, suggesting that some binaries may not reach $q_b=1$ within the $30\,\mathrm{Myr}$ lifetime of a quasar and therefore allowing LISA to constrain the accretion history of SMBBHs. Our findings also suggest that periodically flickering jets are a potential observable signature of many binaries.

</details>


### [105] [Evidence of Orbital Evolution of Gigahertz-Peaked Spectra in Binary Millisecond Pulsar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04731)
*Rahul Sharan,Bhaswati Bhattacharyya*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了双星毫秒脉冲星PSR J2144−5237中GHz峰值谱（Gigahertz-Peaked Spectrum）转折频率的轨道演化。利用Parkes超宽带低频接收机约50小时的密集观测，在704 MHz至4 GHz范围内持续覆盖约10天的轨道周期，探测到两个脉冲分量转折频率随轨道相位的一致调制。这种相关性表明谱变率由双星内部环境的传播效应主导，可通过伴星磁化等离子体中的回旋共振吸收来解释，从而为限制脉冲星风电子密度分布、伴星磁场强度和吸收等离子体几何结构提供了新手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星双星系统中，亿食候选体内部的磁化等离子体环境对射电辐射的传播影响尚不清楚。GHz峰值谱转折频率的轨道演化此前从未被系统研究，作者希望利用这一谱特征作为探测双星内部磁化介质的新型诊断工具，以理解脉冲星风与伴星相互作用。

Method: 使用Parkes超宽带低频接收器，收集约50小时高密度采样观测数据，覆盖脉冲星约10天轨道的完整周期，频率范围从704 MHz到4 GHz连续覆盖。对第一和第二脉冲分量分别测量转折频率随轨道相位的变化，并在回旋共振吸收框架下对数据进行建模，以约束吸收入射体的电子密度分布、伴星磁场强度和系统几何参数。

Result: 检测到两个脉冲分量的GHz峰值谱转折频率均随轨道相位出现清晰且系统的调制，且两个分量的演化趋势高度相似。这种关联演化强烈表明谱变率源于双星内部环境的传播效应。回旋共振吸收模型能够成功解释大振幅的转折频率轨道调制，而其他吸收机制无法重现观测行为。

Conclusion: 该研究将GHz峰值谱的轨道演化确立为探测磁化双星内部介质的新型有力手段，为研究脉冲星风与伴星在亿食毫秒脉冲星双星中的相互作用提供了此前尚未探索的新途径，并可同时约束电子密度分布、伴星磁场强度和吸收等离子体及双星系统的几何结构。

Abstract: We present the first systematic investigation of the continuous orbital evolution of the Gigahertz-Peaked Spectum turnover frequency ($ν_{peak}$) in the binary millisecond pulsar, PSR J2144$-$5237. Using approximately 50 hours of densely sampled observations spanning the $\sim10$-day orbit with the Parkes Ultra-Wideband Low frequency receiver, we obtained continuous frequency coverage from 704 MHz to 4 GHz, enabling robust measurements of the spectral evolution throughout the binary orbit. We detect a clear and systematic modulation of the turnover frequency with orbital phase for both the $1^{st}$ pulse and $2^{nd}$ pulse components, with both emission components exhibiting remarkably similar evolutionary trends despite originating from distinct emission regions within the pulsar magnetosphere. The correlated evolution of the two pulse components strongly suggests that the observed spectral variability is governed by propagation through the intra-binary environment. The large orbital modulation of turnover frequency can be explained by cyclotron resonance absorption in magnetized plasma associated with the companion, while alternative absorption mechanisms are unable to reproduce the observed behaviour. Modelling the orbital evolution of the turnover frequency within the cyclotron absorption framework, can constrain the electron density distribution in the pulsar wind, estimate the magnetic field strength of the companion, and place limits on the geometry of the absorbing plasma and the binary system. This study establish orbital evolution of the Gigahertz Peaked Spectum as a powerful new diagnostic of the magnetized intra-binary medium and provide a hitherto unexplored probe of pulsar wind-companion interactions in eclipsing millisecond pulsar binaries.

</details>


### [106] [Through a glass, darkly: a combined framework for estimating fast radio burst host galaxy and population properties in an era of uncertain host identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04765)
*C. W. James,B. C. Andersen,L. Marnoch,J. L. Hoffmann,N. Loudas,J. X. Prochaska,S. D. Ryder,M. Woodland*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的贝叶斯形式化方法，将zDM代码、PATH统计宿主星系识别工具以及描述FRB固有宿主星系分布的模型相结合，用以处理具有不确定宿主星系关联的快速射电暴（FRB），从而更充分地利用FRB数据并减少对近距亮宿主的偏置。该方法在合成观测中验证了其保真度，即便在30角秒的定位不确定度下也能重现固有宿主星系性质。应用于ASKAP定位的FRB样本后，证实FRB偏好暗于恒星形成所预测的宿主星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法处理宿主星系关联不确定的FRB，导致大量数据被浪费，且可能引入对近距、明亮宿主的系统性偏置，影响基于色散量（DM）的宇宙学与FRB种群性质研究的准确性。

Method: 将三要素整合为统一形式化框架：zDM代码（用于建模Macquart关系的DM-z分布）、PATH（用于统计宿主星系识别）、以及描述FRB固有宿主星系分布的一组模型。通过从星系目录采样模拟宿主来生成合成FRB观测集，验证方法保真度，并应用于ASKAP实际定位的FRB样本。

Result: 在合成数据集上，即使FRB定位误差高达30角秒——传统PATH分析无法给出可靠宿主关联的情况下——该方法仍能准确重现固有宿主星系性质。应用于ASKAP样本时，证实FRB偏好比恒星形成率预测更暗的宿主星系，对应指数表面密度标度为半光半径的0.41^{+0.13}_{-0.09}倍。

Conclusion: 该形式化方法为处理宿主关联不确定的FRB提供了可靠工具，能更充分利用现有数据并消除系统性偏置；其结果与先前研究一致，并鼓励将该方法推广至其他FRB探测设备的数据集。

Abstract: The identification of fast radio burst (FRB) host galaxies, and subsequently their redshifts ($z$), has allowed the FRB dispersion measure (DM) to be used to probe the cosmological distribution of ionised gas, and study the properties of the FRB population itself. However, current methods cannot account for FRBs with uncertain host galaxy associations, leading to underutilisation of data, and potential biases towards nearby, bright hosts. In this work, we develop a methodology which can. We do so by combining three ingredients - the zDM code, for modelling the Macquart relation; PATH, for statistical host galaxy identification; and a set of models able to describe the intrinsic FRB host galaxy distribution - into a single formalism. We prove the fidelity of our formalism by using a synthetic set of FRB observations, with simulated hosts sampled from galaxy catalogues, and show that it reproduces intrinsic host galaxy properties even for FRB localisation uncertainties of 30", where a traditional PATH analysis produces no confidant host associations. When applied to a sample of FRBs localised by the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder, we confirm previous results showing that FRBs prefer host galaxies fainter than that predicted by star-formation, consistent with an exponential surface density scaling as $0.41^{+0.13}_{-0.09}$ times the half-light radius. We encourage the application of this formalism to data-sets from other FRB-hunting instruments.

</details>


### [107] [The angular structure of the GW170817 jet from prompt emission alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04776)
*R. Moradi,R. Ruffini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文仅利用GW170817的前兆（prompt）发射数据，无需余晖拟合或环星星际密度/微物理参数，确定了其喷流角结构。通过与GRB 090510（同类型但近轴观测）对比，并借助引力波数据的支持，得出喷流能量角分布指数n=3.76±0.29，排除多种常用结构模型，并确定喷流在视线方向仍保持相对论性，喷流本质结构而非多普勒增亮所致，同时定出发射半径对引力波-伽马射线延迟的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW170717的伽马射线亮度远低于预期，需要理解其喷流结构以解释这一"暗淡"现象。传统方法依赖余晖拟合及多种不确定参数，本文旨在仅凭前兆发射和引力波独立数据约束喷流角结构，无需自由参数。

Method: 假设GW170817与GRB 090510属同类喷流，分别为干涉角20°和近轴观测；利用点源多普勒标度不适用时的正确关系E_iso=4πε(θ_v)和E_p,i∝Γ(θ_v)，由两暴峰值能量之比直接导出ε-Γ幂律指数n，并结合GRB 090510的核心洛伦兹因子与视角给出角分布ε∝θ^{-7.4}及发射半径。

Result: n=3.76±0.29，无自由参数；排除4种常用喷流结构（4.7σ-13σ），其余3种仍高于4σ；确定核心宽度2°-5°，视线洛伦兹因子Γ=33；角分布指数超出均匀共动流洛伦兹增亮所能产生范围，证明结构是本征的；发射半径贡献0.41s延迟中的1.74s，其余为喷流抛射与突破时间。

Conclusion: GW170817的微弱伽马射线源于喷流本身的内在角结构（能量随角度急剧下降），而非明亮核心的被去集束（de-beaming）效应。该方法完全不依赖余晖或外部介质参数，为喷流结构研究提供了新的独立途径。

Abstract: We determine the angular structure of the GW170817 jet by the prompt emission alone, without afterglow fitting, circumburst density or microphysical parameters. We assume that GRB 090510 and GW170817 have outflows of the same kind, observed respectively on-axis and at the interferometric viewing angle of $20^\circ$. We support this assumption with independent gravitational-wave data showing compatible binary masses and radiated energies. We show that for an observer whose beaming cone is filled with outflow, the point-source Doppler scalings do not apply: $E_{\rm iso}=4πε(θ_v)$, with $ε$ the energy radiated per unit solid angle along the line of sight, while the peak energy follows $E_{\rm p,i}\proptoΓ(θ_v)$. We obtain $n={\rm d}\lnε/{\rm d}\lnΓ= 3.76\pm0.29$ from the ratio of the two bursts, with no free parameter and no assumed angle. This excludes four structures in common use at $4.7σ$ to $13σ$; three remain above $4σ$ across the full reported range of the peak energy of the GW170817 jet. Two prompt spectra fix no angular scale; supplying it with the core Lorentz factor of GRB 090510 and the viewing angle gives $ε\proptoθ^{-7.4}$ outside a core of $2^\circ$--$5^\circ$, in agreement with the width inferred from $367$ short bursts, the outflow remaining relativistic at $Γ=33$ on the line of sight. The exponent exceeds what the Lorentz boost of a uniform comoving flow can produce, so the structure is intrinsic to the outflow and not a consequence of the boost. The same structure fixes the emission radius, which contributes $0.41$~s of the $1.74$~s delay between the gravitational-wave signal and the gamma-rays, the remainder being the launch and breakout of the jet, with no free parameters. We conclude that the faintness lies in the structure of the GW170817 jet, not in the de-beaming of a bright core.

</details>


### [108] [The October 2022 flare in OJ 287 and the mass of its primary black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04998)
*Mauri J. Valtonen,Staszek Zola,Manpreet Singh,Andrei V. Berdyugin,Kari Nilsson,A. Gopakumar,Alok C. Gupta,Tapio Pursimo,Alexandr E. Volvach,Marek Drozdz,Waldemar Ogloza,Rene Hudec,Martin Jelínek,Jan Štrobl,Michal Zejmo,Stefano Ciprini,Daniel E. Reichart,Vladimir V. Kouprianov,Alberto Sadun,Markus Mugrauer,Katsura Matsumoto,Ryo Imazawa,Makoto Uemura,Lankeswar Dey*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文分析了活动星系核OJ 287中2022年10月潮汐耀发的多波段观测数据，证明该耀发符合潮汐耀发预期，支持了此前利用进动率确定的双黑洞系统中主黑洞质量$M_{BH}=(18.35\pm0.05)\times10^9 M_\odot$的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OJ 287是一颗亮blazar天体，其耀发序列可由准开普勒轨道模型解释，耀发源于次级黑洞对主黑洞吸积盘的撞击。轨道进动率可用于推算主黑洞质量。2022年10月的潮汐耀发被认为可以独立验证进动率与主黑洞质量，为此需进行多波段密集观测以验证该预期。

Method: 对2022年10月潮汐耀发进行宽频段多波长观测，将观测结果与潮汐耀发的理论预期（即次级黑洞接近时导致吸积流增加并汇入喷流）进行对比。同时利用氢线强度与黑洞质量的相关性等次级指标进行交叉检验。

Result: 2022年10月的耀发特征符合潮汐耀发的预期模型，从而支持了由进动率得出的主黑洞质量$M_{BH}=(18.35\pm0.05)\times10^9 M_\odot$的估计。次级指标（氢线强度与黑洞质量的相关性）仅能将质量约束在$M_{BH}\sim10^{10}M_\odot$以上，无法给出更精确的值。

Conclusion: 观测证实2022年10月耀发为潮汐耀发，独立支持了OJ 287双黑洞系统中主黑洞质量约为$1.8\times10^{10}M_\odot$的测定结果，验证了准开普勒轨道模型及进动率分析的有效性。

Abstract: The bright blazar OJ~287 has demonstrated a sequence of flares, which are well explained by a quasi-Keplerian orbit model. The flares are associated with the impact of the secondary on the accretion disk of the primary. The orbit must precess in order to produce the correct sequence of flares, and from the precession rate we calculate the mass of the primary. This precession rate gives the mass of the primary $M_{BH} = (18.35\pm0.05) \times 10^9 M_{\odot}$. Two kinds of flares have been identified: direct flares from the impacts, and tidal flares arising from an increased accretion flow into the jet. The precession rate and the primary black hole mass may be independently determined from both sets of flares; the tidal flare of October 2022 was recommended for an intense campaign for this reason. This paper describes these observations over a wide spectral range. We show that the October 2022 flare fits the expectations for a tidal flare and thus supports the earlier determination of the mass of the binary black hole system in OJ 287. The mass of the primary may also be deduced from secondary indicators such as the correlation with the hydrogen line strength and the black hole mass. These studies require that the mass is above $M_{BH} \sim 10^{10} M_{\odot}$, but do not specify the value more exactly.

</details>


### [109] [Nonstationary Stochastic Timing Signatures in the Prompt Gamma-Ray Light Curve of GRB 170817A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05035)
*Lin Xie,Dahai Yan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用变点模型和深度核高斯过程模型,分析了GRB 170817A(引力波事件GW170817对应的伽马暴)在10-300 keV能段的弱余辉伽马射线发射中的时变随机结构。分析基于0.10秒时间分辨率的Fermi/GBM光变曲线,两种变点结构识别出相似的时间协方差转变(约0.27和0.237秒),固定切分比较支持分段特异性协方差演化;深度核模型在1.65-1.75秒处恢复出局部时间形变特征,与伽马射线起始时间1.74秒一致。经确认,该内容不涉及敏感话题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB 170817A是与引力波事件GW170817相关联的短伽马射线暴,其早期伽马射线发射较弱,统计特性复杂。理解其时间依赖的随机结构对揭示致密双星并合后的喷流物理和辐射机制至关重要。传统平稳模型可能无法充分描述该弱信号中潜在的非平稳随机演化,因此需要更精细的统计工具来探测时间相关的协方差结构和局部时序特征。

Method: 采用两种互补的统计建模方法:(1)变点模型(change-point model)用于检测伽马射线光变曲线中协方差结构的转变位置及转变宽度;(2)深度核高斯过程模型(deep-kernel Gaussian process),以阻尼随机游走(DRW)和Matern-3/2为基核,用于恢复光变曲线的局部时间形变特征。分析基于Fermi/GBM在10-300 keV能段的0.10秒分辨率光变曲线,通过贝叶斯证据进行模型比较(Δln Z_split),并对bin宽度(0.10 vs 0.12 s)和能段细分进行稳健性检验。

Result: 两种变点配置分别在引力波并合后0.269秒和0.237秒处识别出协方差转变,10-90%转变宽度分别为0.441和0.393秒。在代表性门控边界t_tr=0.27秒处,四种固定切分配置均获得相对于全曝光平稳Matern-3/2参考模型的正证据增益(最大Δln Z_split=5.72±0.16)。深度核模型在1.65秒(DRW基核)和1.75秒(Matern-3/2基核)处恢复局部时间形变尖峰,与伽马射线起始时间1.74秒的偏移均在0.10秒采样分辨率内。时间bin宽度变化对结果稳定,但能段细分对门控位置的影响大于形变尖峰。残差诊断显示模型捕获了主导时间结构,但仍存在局部残差依赖性。

Conclusion: 门控定义的协方差转变和局部时间形变特征是模型依赖的现象学计时诊断。固定切分比较支持分段特异性协方差演化,但不需要协方差族的变化。这些非平稳结构的统计稳健性和物理起源仍需通过进一步的模拟校准和计数级建模来评估。该研究为理解与引力波相关的短伽马暴的弱余辉发射时序提供了方法论框架。

Abstract: We investigate time-dependent stochastic structure in the weak prompt gamma-ray emission of GRB 170817A using change-point and deep-kernel Gaussian-process (GP) models. The analysis is based on the 10-300 keV Fermi/GBM light curve with 0.10 s time resolution. Two change-point configurations identify similar covariance transitions at 0.269 and 0.237 s after the gravitational-wave merger, with 10-90% transition widths of 0.441 and 0.393 s, respectively. At the representative gate-defined boundary of t_tr = 0.27 s, all four fixed-split assignments yield positive evidence gains over the full-exposure stationary Matern-3/2 reference. The largest gain is Delta ln Z_split = 5.72 +/- 0.16 for the Matern-3/2 to Matern-3/2 assignment, while the gate-matched damped random walk (DRW) to Matern-3/2 assignment gives Delta ln Z_split = 5.50 +/- 0.16. The fixed-split comparison therefore supports segment-specific covariance evolution without requiring a change of covariance family. The deep-kernel models recover localized time-deformation features peaking at 1.65 and 1.75 s after the merger for DRW and Matern-3/2 base kernels, respectively. Their offsets from the adopted gamma-ray onset at 1.74 s are -0.09 and +0.01 s, both within the 0.10 s sampling resolution. The full-band timing features remain stable when the bin width is changed from 0.10 to 0.12 s, whereas energy subdivision produces larger shifts in gate locations than in warp peaks. Residual diagnostics show that the models reproduce the dominant temporal structure, although localized residual dependence remains. We interpret the gate-defined covariance transition and the localized time-deformation feature as model-dependent phenomenological timing diagnostics. Further simulation calibration and count-level modelling are needed to assess the statistical robustness and physical origin of the recovered nonstationary structure.

</details>


### [110] [Exploring the connection between Fast Radio Bursts and binary neutron star mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05209)
*Barbara Patricelli,Maria Grazia Bernardini,Cecilia Sgalletta,Michela Mapelli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过合成BNS并合目录并模拟其可能产生的磁星残留体，比较了BNS并合及其磁星产生的快速射电暴（FRB）速率与CHIME观测到的事件率，发现BNS并合事件对CHIME探测到的FRB（包括重复和非重复）贡献了不可忽略的比例，支持部分FRB可能与双中子星并合相关联，对多信使天文学有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的物理起源仍未知，磁星被认为是可能的起源天体之一，而双中子星（BNS）并合本身与磁星形成相关联。本文旨在量化FRB与BNS并合之间的可能联系，通过比较预测速率与CHIME观测速率来验证这一假设。

Method: 作者结合近期理论预测的BNS并合率作为红移的函数，以及来自银河系BNS测量的中子星质量分布，合成了BNS并合目录。基于不同状态方程（EoS），预测BNS系统终产物为稳定/超质中子星（磁星）或黑洞的数目，并为每个BNS（及其磁星残留体）模拟一个相关联的FRB，估算能被CHIME探测到的数量。

Result: 研究发现，BNS并合率以及BNS产生的磁星率占CHIME探测到的FRB（包括重复与非重复源）的不可忽略的比例。然而，需要额外的形成通道来解释整个FRB群体。

Conclusion: 尽管BNS并合不能解释所有FRB，但确实存在一部分FRB可能与BNS并合真实相关。这一发现对多信使天文学具有深远意义，支持利用当前和未来设施系统性地搜索FRB与来自BNS并合的引力波事件的符合探测。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are highly energetic radio sources whose duration is of the order of milliseconds. The physical origin of these sources is still unknown. Many models suggest magnetars as possible progenitors of FRBs, and this is supported by the association between FRBs and the Galactic magnetar SGR 1935+2154; other proposed progenitors include binary neutron star (BNS) mergers, that are themselves linked to magnetar formation. In this work we investigate the possible connection between FRBs and BNS mergers, including magnetars that might be produced in such events, by comparing the detection rates inferred from synthetic BNS and associated FRB populations with the rates observed by CHIME. We produce a synthetic catalog of BNS mergers by combining recent theoretically predicted BNS merger rate as a function of redshift and the neutron star mass distribution inferred from measurements of Galactic BNSs. Using this catalog we predict the number of BNS systems ending as magnetars (stable or supramassive neutron star) or black holes (formed promptly or after the collapse of a hypermassive neutron star) for different equations of state. We then simulate for each BNS (and therefore for each magnetar remnant) an associated FRB and estimate how many of them can be potentially detected by CHIME. We find that the rate of BNS mergers and the rate of magnetars produced after BNS represents a non-negligible fraction of the FRBs detected by CHIME, both repeating and non-repeating. Although additional formation channels need to be considered to account for the entire population of FRBs, the existence of a fraction of FRBs that might genuinely be associated to BNS mergers has profound implications in the context of multi- messenger astronomy, supporting the systematic searches of coincident detections of FRBs and GWs from a BNS merger with current and future facilities.

</details>


### [111] [Electromagnetic alignment and jet precession around supermassive black holes: Quasi-periodic oscillations in tidal disruption events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05350)
*Pau Amaro-Seoane,Leif Lui,Alejandro Torres-Orjuela,Xian Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用协变电磁角动量转移公式，研究潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）中的准周期振荡与喷流形成机制，推导出电磁反作用对迷你吸积盘产生的逆行进动解析扭矩，并建立可观测的瞬变信号与频率漂移特征的预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解TDE中磁通量累积如何驱动Blandford-Znajek喷流、推断黑洞自旋和磁通密度，并解释准周期振荡的物理起源，需超越光谱连续谱拟合的独立方法。

Method: 采用广义相对论框架下电磁角动量转移的协变表述；对事件视界上的Noether电流密度做小自旋展开，推导出闭合形式的电磁反作用解析扭矩；使用分裂单极子磁场拓扑模拟无力磁层；结合Lense-Thirring进动与电磁反作用诱导的逆行进动进行运动学评估。

Result: 电磁反作用诱导迷你盘逆行进动，与顺行Lense-Thirring进动耦合决定全局振荡频率；预测峰谷通量比单调衰减、频率漂移特征表现为先延长后缩短的耀斑间隔；提出磁通耗尽阻碍对齐机制，预测末期残余调制振幅恒定；建立从频率漂移时间导数提取黑洞自旋与磁通密度的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了从TDE准周期振荡时序信号中直接提取黑洞物理参数的解析框架，为独立于光谱拟合的天体物理测量开辟了新的理论途径。

Abstract: We evaluate quasi-periodic oscillations and jet formation in tidal disruption events using the covariant formulation of electromagnetic angular-momentum transfer. General-relativistic frame-dragging tears apart misaligned transient accretion flows, forming an isolated inner mini-disk. The accumulation of magnetic flux on the event horizon powers a relativistic jet via the Blandford-Znajek mechanism. Because the magnetic field anchors to the precessing mini-disk, the jet axis rotates, generating geometric modulations in the observed X-ray and radio fluxes. To ensure physical consistency with the force-free magnetosphere required to launch a Blandford-Znajek jet, we model the electromagnetic back-reaction using a split-monopole magnetic field topology. By performing a small-spin expansion of the Noether current density over the event horizon, we derive a closed-form analytical reaction torque exerted by the electromagnetic field on the accretion plasma. We evaluate the resulting kinematics to show that the electromagnetic back-reaction induces a retrograde precession of the mini-disk, coupling with the prograde Lense-Thirring precession to dictate the global oscillation frequency. We formulate explicit predictions for observable transient signals and predict a monotonic attenuation of the peak-to-trough flux ratio as the mini-disk aligns, as well as a specific frequency drift signature characterized by an initial lengthening followed by an asymptotic shortening of the time interval between consecutive flares. We establish an analytical mechanism where magnetic flux depletion stalls alignment, predicting a constant residual modulation amplitude at late times. We formulate a methodology to extract the black hole spin and the magnetic flux density directly from the temporal derivatives of this predicted frequency drift, operating independently of spectral continuum fitting.

</details>
